# Paxyo Chat-Order Bot — Project Handover

**Date:** 13 November 2026, ~23:54 EAT (Africa/Addis_Ababa) — updated 14 Nov 10:08 EAT (Rounds 8/8.1 live, Qwen 401, next batch queued)
**Owner:** William (Yohannes Abate, abatejohannes@gmail.com)
**Purpose of this file:** everything the next Muse needs to understand and continue the @paxorder_bot project. Nothing else (no Paxyo admin panel, no Dashenbet, no Novavend).

---

## 1. What the bot is

- **Bot:** @paxorder_bot, display name "Paxyo Order", https://t.me/paxorder_bot
- **What it does:** users order social-media services (followers, likes, views, comments, subscribers) by chatting — text AND voice, in Amharic or English. First-time chatters get a Paxyo account auto-created on /start.
- **Money rule (owner's core rule):** "the AI only listens and talks. All money steps are deterministic code." The server computes all totals; the AI never sets prices.
- **Fulfilment:** Paxyo talks to the provider GodOfPanel (godofpanel.com) via paxyo.et. **The bot NEVER talks to GodOfPanel directly, and must NEVER reveal GodOfPanel to users, never reveal what Paxyo pays for services, and never call it an SMM panel service.** Only Paxyo selling prices are shown. If asked about provider/source/cost, deflect politely without confirming or denying.
- **Pricing:** Paxyo sell price = provider rate × **420 ETB** (confirmed by William; NOT ×400). Owner buys 1 USD for 200 ETB, sells at 420, profit 220 ETB per 1 USD. The 420 multiplier is editable in Paxyo Settings.
- **Blacklisted services, permanently:** **5379 and 5834** — never sell them, never surface them in cheapest-first lists.
- **Customer bot vs admin bot:** customer bot is @paxyo_bot; admin bot is @paxadmin_bot (owned by Abel, NOT part of this project). This project is ONLY @paxorder_bot.

## 3. The Paxyo Bot API (on paxyo.et)

Built on paxyo.et (PHP + live MySQL), NOT on Abel's Render backend. File: `public_html/api/bot.php` (+ `.htaccess` rewrite `api/bot/(.*)` → `api/bot.php?bot_path=$1`).

- **Auth:** header `X-Bot-Secret`, value is `BOT_API_SECRET` stored in `config_secret.php` on the server. **Value never recorded anywhere — never put it in chat, git, memory or files.**
- **Rate limit:** 30 requests/min per telegram_id (verified: 35 rapid calls → 28×200 then 7×429).
- **Endpoints (all verified live):**
  - `GET /api/bot/services` — 7,176 services, price in ETB per 1000, rate_multiplier 420, fields: id, provider_service_id, name, category, type, price, currency, min, max, average_time, active. Blacklisted services listed with active:false. Service 429 sold as 9875 ("30 Days" → "Lifetime") at flat 13.20 ETB/1000.
  - `GET /api/bot/balance?telegram_id=` — balance in ETB.
  - `POST /api/bot/orders` — validates account/service/link/quantity (multiple of 10, min/max), refuses blacklisted (403) and disabled services, holiday discount, server-side total, atomic balance debit, provider order via GodOfPanel, **idempotency_key (UNIQUE column on orders)** — duplicate key returns the earlier order with `duplicate:true`, no second charge.
  - `GET /api/bot/orders/{id}?telegram_id=` — by local id or provider api_order_id, scoped to user.
  - `GET /api/bot/orders?telegram_id=&limit=` — history, newest first, max 20.
  - `POST /api/bot/users` — auto-create on /start: {telegram_id, username, first_name} → created true/false. Identity: `auth.tg_id` IS the Telegram ID.
  - Recurring: `POST /api/bot/recurring` (interval_minutes ≥ 30), `GET /api/bot/recurring`, `POST /api/bot/recurring/{id}/cancel|pause|ran`, `GET /api/bot/recurring/due` (secret-only). Table `recurring_orders`.
  - Deposits: `POST /api/bot/deposits` (Chapa Direct Charge / USSD push), `GET /api/bot/deposits/{id}` (re-verifies with Chapa; credit once only on amount match; failed/rejected/cancelled or >48h still-pending → Failed), `GET /api/bot/deposits` list. Min 10 ETB, max 100,000 ETB. Method/phone stored as JSON in `chapa_response` (deposits table has no method/phone columns).
- **⚠️ CHAPA DIRECT CHARGE BLOCKED:** Chapa refused the live test with "You are not allowed for direct charges, please contact us!" — Paxyo's Chapa account is NOT enabled for direct charges. **Decision pending from William:** (a) ask Chapa to enable direct charges, or (b) wire the checkout-link fallback (`/transaction/initialize`) into POST /api/bot/deposits. Until then, deposit-by-chat USSD push cannot work; the bot must not promise it works. Test deposit row id 1204 was created then DELETED.
- **M-Pesa:** the bot LISTS M-Pesa as an accepted payment method (Telebirr, CBE Birr, CBE, M-Pesa), but deposit-by-chat has NO M-Pesa path yet. Needs a follow-up payment path.

- **Amharic translate-bridge (NEW, live in 5548ee1):** when a message contains Ethiopic script, Gemini translates it to English first (links, numbers, service IDs, prices preserved), the normal flow runs in English, then the prose reply is translated back to Amharic. English never touches the bridge. If either translation fails, fall back to the original / send English — never go silent, never drop the order. Confirm cards and buttons are NEVER translated. The bridge uses the existing Gemini key (no new key needed).
- **Amharic warning (all Chinese models):** a 2026 benchmark found Qwen3 near-zero on Amharic and DeepSeek-V3.2 also collapsed on African languages; only Gemini held up on low-resource languages. Any new provider MUST be live-tested in Amharic before becoming primary. The bridge mitigates this.

- **Quality ranking for this bot's job (owner asked 23:51 EAT):** 1. Groq gpt-oss-120b (smartest + fastest, proven in all rounds), 2. DeepSeek, 3. Qwen-Turbo (cheapest, weakest — smallest model; good for clear messages, weaker on messy/typo-heavy ones). Qwen has NOT been live-tested yet — test it with messy messages once its key is live. Bigger option if Qwen-Turbo is too weak: Qwen-Plus ($0.40/$1.20).

## 5. What the bot can do (behaviour, all tested)

- **Ordering:** intent classification → service pick (fastest by average time, or user-named service via service ID) → Confirm card (service/quantity/link/total) → place → summary. **No order without an explicit Confirm tap — EXCEPT urgent "please order now"** (owner override, round 5: explicit phrases only, places at once, full summary after).
- **Short names only** when showing services for selection; full/long name ONLY when the user entered a service ID.
- **Price questions answered directly** — never ask for the link first. Budget answers state quantities (e.g. "10 birr ≈ 520 TikTok views").
- **Speed questions:** real estimate from the pending/fastest service's average time + "sooner we place it, sooner it completes — let's order now". The vague "shown before you confirm / varies by service" wording is gone.
- **Reply-to recognised:** replying "1000" to "How many?" works; "yes" to a Confirm card confirms.
- **Memory:** last ~10 exchanges/user, persisted to chat_history.json (Render free disk is ephemeral on redeploy). "Same as usual" reuses last service + link + quantity. "Same as yesterday but double" caps at max.
- **Edge cases (owner spec, 13 Nov 16:26):** "the usual"; "boost my latest video" (ask for link); budget-first; goal-based (ask which metric); split across N posts (one Confirm places all); multi-service single message (batch Confirm); drip-feed = recurring ≥30 min (<30 refused in one line); friend's typo'd handle (exact handle on Confirm); platform mismatch (flagged, NEVER placed mismatched); shortened links accepted; private/expiring links (one warning on Confirm, never rejected); mid-order change/cancel (draft editable/cancellable, placed = show status); impatience (real status + average time, never "soon"); short delivery (status first, NO refill/refund promises); receipt screenshot mid-order → deposit recheck, half-built order survives; split payment NOT supported (deposit first); reverse-calc quantity from balance; NO discount negotiation; NO geo-targeting; NO free trial (cheapest service suggested); prompt injection on price ignored; caption/posting-time tips brief OK; website problems → support redirect.
- **Ban/shadowban question:** answer NO — "anybody can buy followers for you, so your account can't be banned or shadowbanned for it without your permission" (William's answer, supersedes the old no-zero-risk line).
- **"Real Ethiopian followers":** global network, never overpromise.
- **Terms:** refunds ONLY to Paxyo balance (never bank/mobile money), balance cannot be withdrawn as cash, orders cannot be cancelled/modified once processing starts, accounts must be public, results not guaranteed permanent, drops may occur. Source: paxyo-terms.md.
- **Payments accepted:** Telebirr, CBE Birr, CBE, M-Pesa. First-deposit bonus 20% with invite code REF3723FB335. Bot must remind users the account must NOT be private (on every Confirm card).
- **Deposits by chat:** Confirm card first, then Chapa USSD push (method + amount + phone only). If push fails → checkout-link fallback (pending the Chapa decision in §3). Deposit recheck: user says "I paid" or sends a receipt screenshot → bot re-verifies with Chapa, reports paid / still waiting / failed, never invents a reason.
- **Recurring orders:** "every 3 hours 1k views" etc. — bot creates the schedule itself via the API; 60s scheduler runs due ones. Known gap: recurring flow does NOT yet honour a named service_id (falls back to fastest pick).
- **Photos:** bot reads uploaded pictures (payment screenshot, profile screenshot, error screenshot) via vision, 10 MB cap, bytes in memory only.
- **Voice input:** transcribes Amharic/English voice messages and SHOWS the user what they said ("You said: ..."), then handles it like typed text. **Voice-order claims were removed from greeting/help in round 5** ("since we dont now") — re-add only after a live voice test proves it works (never testable from cloud/device browsers — no microphone).
- **Voice replies (SPEAKING): DROPPED by William 13 Nov 14:35 EAT.** Bot answers in text only. Do NOT resume TTS work unless he re-raises it. (Gemini TTS cannot speak Amharic via API — empty audio; Google Cloud TTS setup stopped, $30 prepayment declined, nothing paid, no key created.)
- **GIFs/stickers:** understood and replied to (caption read as message). Humour: sometimes jokes, sends GIFs/stickers. General knowledge: brief answers, steers back to ordering after 2–3 off-topic chats. Offensive users: firm pushback (never polite/apologetic, no slurs back), 2nd refusal, 3rd+ silent; any normal message resets.
- **Paxyo facts the bot knows:** Ethiopian service for followers/likes/views/comments/subscribers (TikTok, Instagram, Telegram, YouTube, Facebook, Twitter/X); owner Yohannes Abate/William; website paxyo.et; channel t.me/paxyo251; customer bot @paxyo_bot; mini app https://miniapp-one-1.onrender.com/; support support@paxyo.et; pay in ETB from Paxyo balance, deposit Telebirr/CBE via Chapa; prices ETB per 1000.
- **Comment Likes:** TikTok Comment Likes = likes ON a comment, distinct from video likes and comments. Bot offers #5854 for "likes for my comment".
- **"Use the fastest one" button:** offered on the "Which one do you want — views, likes?" question (round 7).
- **Order history:** /orders shows a monospace table (ID, service, quantity, price, status) with ETB labels, up to 20, newest first. Bot orders DO appear in the user's mini-app order history (verified 13 Nov 21:33 — orders 1828/1829 show as provider orders #77968996/#77971743, COMPLETED).

## 7. Round history — what each round fixed

- **Round 3 (0aa7c9b, live 20:19):** root cause — Groq 404 on llama-3.3-70b-versatile (model gone for free accounts) + Gemini 429 → local fallback picked Comment Likes for views. Fix: GROQ_MODEL=openai/gpt-oss-120b, metric-from-text safety net, Confirm-card questions answered from pending service, secrets scrubbed from logs.
- **Round 4 (564352e, live 20:53):** budget answers state quantities; speed from pending service; quantity change updates Confirm in place reusing link; cheapest stays on platform/metric; bot taught Paxyo facts; video/comment likes/comments distinguished.
- **Round 5 (b82456b, live 22:11):** fastest-one fallback respects stored metric; bare quantity replies; paxyo.et mentioned; short service names; price answered directly; reply-to; orders table; voice-order claims removed; voice fix (transcription shown); GIFs/stickers; humour; general knowledge + steer-back; offensive-user pushback; real speed estimates + order-now nudge; memory; "same as usual"; urgent order-now override; ban = NO; M-Pesa listed; public-account reminder; terms studied.
- **Round 6 (3dab7e3, live ~22:51):** Cancel clears the stored draft; same-as-usual reuses the link; link mismatch explained in one line; "how can I pay?" lists methods first; ETB labels on totals; ban answer covers shadowban.
- **Round 7 (3374b1f, live 23:26):** state leakage killed (Amharic metric words read; new metric replaces stored draft; cancelled record clears only when an order is placed); order-now quantity read from before the metric ("200 TikTok views"); mismatch timing (local order-request guard forces intent "order" when a link + metric + order verb are present); fastest-one button on the metric question.
- **Providers + bridge (5548ee1, live 23:32):** Qwen-Turbo provider (2nd), GLM fallback (last), Amharic translate-bridge via Gemini. Chain: DeepSeek → Qwen → Groq → OpenAI → Gemini → GLM. 472/472 tests, no existing test changed.
- **Round 8 (3c63697, live 14 Nov 09:24):** confirm-understanding restatement, emoji reading, free chat, greeting dedupe. 498/498 tests. Retest 09:29–09:37: PARTIAL PASS — "Do you understand" now answered correctly ("Yeah, I got it — you want 👍 reactions for your Telegram channel post") but forgot the 1,000 and asked "how many" again; free chat ignored "I'm tired"; emoji-only 👍 got only the stock link ask; PRICE BUG: same 👍 wording quoted 336.00 ETB (#1758 Auto reactions, 10 future posts) and 81.90 (#216 Bot Start) — both wrong; right 👍 service is Thumbs up/Like, e.g. #6541 at 5.46 ETB/1000.
- **Round 8.1 (9c790f0, live 14 Nov 09:58):** restatement retains quantity (`_remember_price_question`, `_recent_quantity`); bare-emoji acknowledgement (`emoji_ack_order`/`emoji_ack_fresh`); feelings first in free chat (`_feeling_ack`); deterministic reactions matching (`pick_services(reaction_emoji=...)`, `_reactions_pool` — emoji family only, Bot Start + Auto-future-posts excluded by name; short names keep the reaction segment). 511/511 tests. **Retest 10:01–10:07 EAT: ALL 6 CHECKS PASS** — 👍 price now **5.46 ETB for 1,000** (Thumbs up); "Do you understand" restated 👍 + channel post + 1,000, no "how many"; tired feeling acknowledged; emoji-only 👍 acknowledged as the reaction choice; "what do you mean" explained well. No order placed.
- **Next batch (9cc76be, live 14 Nov 10:18):** reseller intent → "contact William at t.me/paxyo"; contact/support intent → "You can reach William (Yohannes Abate) at t.me/paxyo"; never-drop claim — ONLY Telegram reactions, Telegram views, Facebook reactions ("{service} never drop — once they are delivered, they stay."), hooked in the direct-answer check, the Confirm-card drop answer and the draft drop answer; negative tests keep the terms answer for everything else. 546/546 tests. **Retest 10:25–10:31 EAT: ALL 7 CHECKS PASS** — reseller + support both point to t.me/paxyo; all three never-drop claims correct; TikTok followers and Instagram likes did NOT get the never-drop claim. No order placed. NOTE: Paxyo's Terms say "results not guaranteed permanent, drops may occur" — the never-drop line goes against that; William was told 14 Nov in case he wants the terms updated.
- **Paused services (6e649d2, live 14 Nov 10:32):** CapCut Pro, Canva Pro, cheap premium subscriptions and SMS OTP verification are PAUSED — bot answers "That one is paused right now — we will let you know when it's ready." (Amharic version too). Only when asked, never volunteered; no date, no price. Guard is deliberately narrow: "premium" is a live catalogue word (293 "premium" services, mostly Telegram Premium members; Spotify followers/likes are real orderable services) — the guard matches brand names (capcut, canva, netflix, spotify premium, youtube premium, premium subscription) and OTP phrasing, but NOT "premium followers", "Spotify followers", "Telegram Premium". 565/565 tests. **Retest 10:38–10:40 EAT: ALL 5 CHECKS PASS** — CapCut Pro, Spotify premium, SMS OTP, Canva Pro all got the paused answer; Spotify followers behaved as a normal order (asked for the link, NOT paused). Flow cancelled after. No order placed.

## 14 Nov 13:58 EAT — Language round + price fast path (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **BUILT + VERIFIED 13:58 EAT (626/626 tests, verified by me: 615 baseline + 11 new).** Not pushed/deployed — William is testing the bot live now (voice note); a deploy would restart the service mid-test. Push+deploy after he reports his test.
- **Stuck-language bug — ROOT CAUSE FOUND:** the old `_BRIDGE_AMHARIC` flag was set True on any Amharic turn and NEVER reset, and updates run sequentially (one at a time), so the flag leaked into every later turn. Any Amharic turn (incl. William's "Selam", normalized to fidels) poisoned all later English replies. Second bug: `lang_of` fell back to the Telegram account language even for plainly-English messages.
- **Fix:** new `detect_language(text, user_lang)` → am/ti/om/en (Ge'ez script = Amharic or Tigrinya by vocabulary; Latin + Oromo keyword = Oromo; else English; account language only when the message carries no signal). Bridge now per-turn (`_BRIDGE_LANG`, reset every turn); Gemini translate prompts parameterized by language; `t()` falls back to English instead of KeyError. First-pass Oromo/Tigrinya translations added for short deterministic lines only (greeting, contact/reseller, paused, never-drop) — FLAGGED unreviewed by a native speaker; bridge is the fallback. /start greeting uses detect_language.
- **Price delay — DIAGNOSIS:** the 1:35:18 greeting was almost certainly the LATE answer to William's own "Selam" (1:33), not Check 1's reply. Check 1's price DID arrive (1:36:13) but 68s late. Two delay sources: sequential update queue (earlier messages ahead of Check 1) + price answer waited on the DeepSeek classifier and the bridge translate. The price fix itself worked; the retest FAIL was a misattribution.
- **Fix:** price questions now answered from local detection only, BEFORE the classifier runs (fast path + one cached catalogue fetch); falls through to the classifier + existing price branch if it can't name a service. `_price_line` takes the platform from the pending order/draft when the question names a metric but no platform.
- **TikTok-likes pick CONFIRMED:** live 1,185.05 ETB/5,000 (237.01/1000) matches real services #5812/#2374 ("Instant Start") in the 12 Nov snapshot — fastest-first, not a bug. Cheapest #4019 = 6.30/1000 (31.50/5,000); #5379 (5.88) blacklisted, never picked. Test pins fastest = 1,185.05.
- **Not yet verified live:** real Gemini Oromo/Tigrinya translation quality (mocked offline); first-pass t() translations unreviewed; 68s delay end-to-end (fast path removes the classifier, but a slow turn queued ahead can still delay — `concurrent_updates` deliberately NOT changed, would race shared per-user order state); TikTok fastest pick vs LIVE average times (backend fetch timed out, not retried).

## 14 Nov 14:08 EAT — Widen the bot: DeepSeek answers freely (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **William 14:01 EAT:** bot does NOT reply when users ask how-to questions ("how do I get the link of a Telegram post?"), though DeepSeek itself would answer. He had asked before to "give them more space, make them widen" and it wasn't done. First brief: build 100+ canned Q&As. **COURSE CORRECTION 14:04 EAT (William):** do NOT prepare hundreds of questions — let DeepSeek answer them freely; only when out of subject, answer it, then bring them back to ordering.
- **Silence — ROOT CAUSE FOUND (reproduced in a test first):** "how do I get the link of a Telegram post?" has NO home in the classifier's intent list, so it lands as `off_topic` (or `paxyo_info`, whose prompt told the AI to answer "using only these facts" and steer back for anything outside Paxyo — the facts say nothing about Telegram links). The off-topic path gave a free general answer only on the FIRST 2 turns; from the 3rd turn on, the bot sent only the canned steer line ("I'm the Paxyo order bot — I can only help with Paxyo orders.") forever, until another intent reset the counter. William testing the same question repeatedly hit the cap immediately.
- **Fix:** new `how_to` intent in the SYSTEM_PROMPT (asks how to do something needed to order or use a platform — how to get a post/comment link, how to make an account public… answer from general knowledge, never deflect) + `how_to` branch with a free AI answer and NO steer-back line (it's about ordering). Off-topic path widened: a real question (new `is_question()` — ends with `?` or opens with a question word) ALWAYS gets a free AI answer + ONE short steer-back line ("By the way — what would you like to order?"), however many turns; questions don't count toward the cap. Only non-question chit-chat is capped (2 turns, then the steer). `paxyo_info` prompt widened: how-to about getting a link / making an account public / using Telegram, TikTok or Instagram is answered from the AI's own general knowledge; only truly unrelated questions get the one-line steer.
- **Deterministic Paxyo facts ONLY (nothing else canned):** prices (already deterministic); **comment likes — NEW:** `is_comment_likes_question()` + `t("comment_likes_need")` (en + am) — "the link of the video or post the comment is on, and either the link of the comment itself or your username"; a real order (link pasted) never triggers it and goes through the order flow. Private-account rule, refunds-to-balance, reseller/contact → t.me/paxyo, paused services were already deterministic and are untouched. Secrecy guardrails untouched; language work untouched (Amharic bridge still translates replies back).

Push+deploy TOGETHER with the language round + price fast path after he reports his voice test.

Amharic/Oromo/Tigrinya via the bridge; the `how_to` intent in live classification (DeepSeek may still label some how-tos `off_topic` or `paxyo_info` — both paths now answer freely, so either way the user gets an answer); first-pass Amharic wording of `comment_likes_need` unreviewed by a native speaker.

## 14 Nov 14:59 EAT — Owner fix round: 5 faults (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **William tested the live bot himself (14:35 EAT) and found 4 faults; a 5th arrived with screenshots 14:37–14:40 EAT.** Built in `~/workspace/paxyo-bot`, verified offline. NOT pushed/deployed — push+deploy only after the parent confirms.
- **Fault 1 — NEVER MORALIZE ABOUT FAKE FOLLOWERS.** DeepSeek's free answers said things like "avoid buying fake followers". Fix: SYSTEM_PROMPT rule (never warn against buying followers/likes/views/reactions, never call them "fake" — Paxyo sells these; if asked whether they are real, say they come from Paxyo's network) + `strip_moralizing()` sanitizer on `answer_general_question` and `answer_paxyo_question` replies (drops any sentence matching the moralizing regexes). Safety/ban questions keep the ESTABLISHED answer only: "No — anybody can buy followers for you, so you can't be banned without your permission."
- **Fault 2 — PRICE STILL MISSING WHEN ASKED.** Owner's own test got no price; my 14:19 retest DID show 5.46 ETB for the thumbs-up wording. **Diagnosed causes:** (a) `is_price_question` missed shapes — "what's the rate", "how much for 1000" (no platform), "how much is it" (the word check compared "it" against "it?" and missed on punctuation), and Amharic when the translate bridge fails (Gemini nearly out of its 20/day limit, so Ge'ez text reaches the detector unchanged); (b) `price_answer_text` returned None when no service could be named, and the question fell through to a free AI reply — whose prompts say "Never quote a price". **Fix:** `is_price_question` broadened (rate/rates/pricing/charge/charges; Amharic ዋጋ + ስንት with a metric/platform; "how much" + a number; "how much is it/this/that/one/them" via word-boundary regex); `_service_price_line` helper; `price_answer_text` rewritten — fetches once, cheapest-service fallback when no fastest pick, never returns None for a price question except when the catalogue fetch itself fails (then falls through to the classifier and retries). Price detection runs BEFORE the free-answer paths, and the post-classification price branch still catches what the fast path can't name.
- **Fault 3 — WRONG TELEGRAM POST-LINK INSTRUCTIONS.** Bot said "click the post and click copy link" (skips the real step); my 14:19 retest answer was desktop-flavoured ("three-dot menu or right-click" — right-click is useless on a phone). **Verified correct steps** (dslink.me/blog/telegram-post-link.html, techwiser.com/how-to-copy-telegram-profile-group-channel-message-links/): on a phone, open the channel/group, find the post, tap the **share arrow** next to the post (or press and hold the post), then tap **"Copy Link"**; link looks like `t.me/channelname/1234`; only public channels/groups have a post link (private ones don't). Encoded as deterministic `telegram_post_link` template (en + am) with a pre-classifier fast path AND a check in the direct-answers block, so the classifier can never route it to a free AI reply. The AI is never called for this question.
- **Fault 4 — ORDER HISTORY UGLY AND CONFUSING.** Was a 20-row monospace table. Now: ONLY THE LAST 5 ORDERS, one line per order — order ID, short service name, quantity, status with icon, total in ETB; newest first; no monospace block. `recent_orders(telegram_id, limit=5)` already defaulted to 5.
- **Fault 5 — WRONG REACTION PICK (screenshots 14:37–14:40 EAT).** Owner asked for 10 🙏 reactions on https://t.me/godofpanelcom/768; bot picked #1734 Positive mix (37.80 ETB/1000, min 50) instead of a 🙏-only service; correcting "I want only 🙏 reaction" showed the SAME #1734 card again. Quantity limits also changed between turns (50–1,000,000, then 100–100,000,000) without naming the service. **Root causes:** (a) 🙏 was NOT in `REACTION_EMOJI` (deliberate — an existing test pins `reaction_emoji("thank you 🙏")` is None) and NOT in `REACTION_FAMILY_WORDS`, so 🙏 was invisible to the reaction machinery and the request fell back to the Positive mix; `_reactions_pool` fell back to the whole pool when no family matched. (b) The draft WAS kept in PARTIAL (complete_order stores it first), but the re-pick is fastest-first by average time and the classifier can re-infer a different service from history — the 100–100M limits match TikTok Video Views services (e.g. #1665), i.e. the re-pick wandered; `bad_quantity` named no service, so the change made no sense to the user. **Fix:** `reaction_emoji` is now context-aware — 🙏 counts only in a reactions context ("reaction"/"telegram"/"tg" in the message); bare "thank you 🙏" still returns None. `REACTION_FAMILY_WORDS` adds 🙏 → ("pray",) — #1723 is the only "pray" match (12.60 ETB/1000, min 10, max 100000) — plus 👎 → ("thumbs down", "dislike"), 😡 → ("angry face",), 😎 → ("cool",), 🤔 → ("thinking",), 🎉 → ("celebration",), 😍 → ("heart eyes",); those emoji added to `REACTION_EMOJI`. `_reactions_pool`: a specific emoji with no family service now returns [] (honest "I don't have a {emoji}-only service — the closest is the Positive mix") instead of the whole pool; a service named with the emoji alone ("Telegram Reactions 👍 | Fast") also counts as that emoji's service if it carries no other reaction emoji. New `try_reaction_correction` helper (used from `handle_order_text` AND `handle_change_order`, so the classifier's label can't swallow it): a different reaction emoji while a Confirm card is pending re-picks the service for that emoji, shows a NEW card, and DROPS the old card so its buttons can't place the old service (complete_order never drops stale pending cards — that was the "same card again" mechanism). `bad_quantity` now names the service ("For {service}, quantity must be between {min} and {max}...") at all 5 call sites + the batch path.
- **BUILT + VERIFIED 14:59 EAT (654/654 tests: 636 baseline + 18 new TestOwnerFixRound14Nov).** 7 failures + 1 error on the first run, all fixed in the round: (1) `is_price_question("how much is it?")` needed the word-boundary regex; (2) the new empty-pool rule broke the `ReactionsPaxyo` fixture ("Telegram Reactions 👍 | Fast" — emoji-only name) → single-emoji-name matching added; (3) `strip_moralizing` returns "" when nothing is left (no `_MORALIZING_FALLBACK` constant — test corrected); (4) `test_off_topic_questions_always_answered` used the link question as its 3rd turn — now deterministic, replaced with "What is the capital of Spain?"; (5) Amharic link template says "share" lowercase. Existing tests updated deliberately: `test_orders_table_newest_first_short_names` (no ``` block, title + 2 lines), `test_link_howto_repro_answered_on_third_turn` (deterministic, AI never called), `test_how_to_intent_gets_free_answer_without_steer` (now "how do I find my TikTok username?"), `test_pending_note_states` (link-only partial-note rule). Sent to the running fix round; if not taken, a fresh round follows.
- **❤️-MAPPING + BAN REVERT + FACEBOOK + LINK/VOICE/MEDIA ROUND — BUILT + VERIFIED 14 Nov 17:5x EAT (695/695 tests: 668 baseline + 27 new TestOwnerFixRound14Nov). NOT pushed/deployed yet — push+deploy only after the parent confirms.** (1) **❤️ root cause:** `_reactions_pool` matched the "heart" family by SUBSTRING, so the ❤️ pool contained Heart Eyes #1725, #1750, "Heart on fire" #6536 and "Broken heart" #6539 as well as Heart #6537, and fastest-first then picked #1725 (its live average time was shorter). The offline fixture never modelled #1725, so tests passed while live failed. **Fix:** exact-emoji rule — a service matches an emoji only when the known emoji in its name are exactly one and it is that emoji; word fallback only when the name has no emoji, whole-word + no other family's word; within one exact emoji the CHEAPEST wins (❤️ → #6537, 😍 → #1725, 👍 → #6541, 🔥 → #6540, 🙏 → #1723); `try_reaction_correction` derives the platform from the pending service instead of hardcoding telegram; 😆 added to the reaction maps; 💔 added so "Broken heart" can't fall into ❤️. Facebook extension: same rule on Facebook services (❤️ → #5105, 👍 → #5923, 😆 → #4417; mixes excluded; no Facebook 🙏 service → honest "don't have" answer). (2) **Ban revert:** template ban_yes → ban_no (en + am), platform-general NO wording; SYSTEM_PROMPT rule, call site, sanitizer comment and all ban tests updated; TRAINING.md carries the full flip history. (3) **Custom link questions:** `ask_link_text(platform, service_hint, lang)` — followers → "{platform} profile link", video views → video link, Telegram post interaction → "{platform} post link", likes/comments → post/video link, comment likes → existing two-thing question; generic ask_link only when the type is genuinely unknown. Wired into all 4 ask_link call sites (recurring, quantity change, budget choice, order flow). 15 existing tests updated deliberately to the new specific wording. (4) **Link recall:** `is_link_recall_question` (the "link of" phrasing separates it from the order flow's "send the link"), `link_recall_text` — GET /orders DOES return `link`, so the bot answers with the actual link of the most recent matching order (platform + kind matched by `_platform_hint_of_service`/`kind_from_text`); no match or no link saved → honest "couldn't find" reply, never an invented link. Hook runs before the orders-history table. (5) **Voice language bug — DIAGNOSED + FIXED:** root cause in our code, confirmed by reading the path: `groq_transcribe` (and openai/gemini) sent NO `language` field, so Whisper auto-detected and mis-detected short Amharic clips as Arabic (both Semitic; Whisper's Amharic is weak); `detect_language` has no Arabic branch, so the Arabic text fell back to English and the AI mirrored Arabic back. Nothing ever produced Arabic deliberately (no "ar" in the bridge, no Arabic templates). Fix: optional `language` param on all transcribe functions + `ai_transcribe`; `transcribe_voice_with_retry` — auto-detect first (behaviour unchanged for normal speech), retry once with a hint (LAST_LANG per-user store, else Telegram language, else "am") ONLY when the first transcription is Arabic-script; if the retry is still Arabic, return "" → honest transcribe_failed. Guard in `handle_order_text`: Arabic-script text never reaches the classifier/AI — honest "please send it in English or Amharic" reply. LAST_LANG is set on every detected turn. (6) **Context-aware media replies:** `media_reply`/`media_order_context`/`media_reply_text` — mood from the emoji (bare emoji or sticker emoji); a GIF carries no emoji so the situation sets the mood (delayed/failed → frustrated, completed → happy); situation from the real order state (most recent placed order's status + average time via the same lookup as the "why hasn't it started" answer, pending Confirm card, half-built draft, or nothing). 2–3 rotating variants per mood × situation per language (per-user counter, so the same user never gets the same line twice); never "soon"; never invents an order; never touches PARTIAL/PENDING. Photos unchanged (Gemini vision, existing test covers the draft surviving). **Not yet verified live:** the whole round end-to-end after deploy — 10 🙏 → Pray card, then "I want only ❤️ reaction" → new Heart #6537 card, old card gone; ban question en + am → new NO answer; Facebook ❤️ flow; custom link questions per type; link recall by voice and by text; Amharic voice note → Amharic reply, never Arabic; frustrated GIF with a delayed order; happy GIF after a completed order; sticker reply.

- **Fault 1 — BARE QUANTITY+EMOJI NOT UNDERSTOOD. Root cause:** "10 🙏" has no home in the flow — `reaction_emoji` returns None for 🙏 without context words ("reaction"/"telegram"/" tg "), the bare-emoji branch needs the message to BE just the emoji, and the classifier calls it unclear. The bare link then had no draft, so the flow asked "Which platform?". **Fix:** new `bare_reaction_order(text)` → (emoji, quantity) for "10 🙏", "🙏 10", "10 👍" and bare "🙏" (a bare emoji already in REACTION_EMOJI keeps the existing branch on its own); new branch in `handle_order_text` (before the media branch, skipped while a Confirm card is pending) starts a Telegram reactions draft (platform telegram, hint "telegram reactions", emoji, quantity) and asks "Please send the link of the Telegram post." The bare link after it completes the draft — merged takes platform/hint/emoji/qty from PARTIAL — and shows the Pray #1723 card, never "Which platform?".
- **Fault 2 — AMHARIC BAN QUESTION WRONG ANSWER. Root cause:** `is_ban_question` only matched Latin words (ban/banned/shadowban). The bridge translates Amharic → English first, but Gemini's free tier is 20/day, so the question reached the detector untranslated and missed — then the classifier answered it freely. **Fix:** the detector now also matches Ge'ez ban stems — ታገድ/ታገደ/ታገዳ/ታግዷ/ታግደ/እገዳ (every real inflection of ታገደ: ሊታገድ, ታገደ, ይታገዳል, ታግዷል, ታግደዋል) and ባን as a whole word — plus Oromo cufamuu/dhoowwaa. The stems are matched exactly: "ታገ" alone would also catch ታገሠ (patience), and the first version of this fix used "ታግድ" (with ድ) which does NOT match the perfect "ታግዷል" (with ዷ) — two tests caught it before the suite passed. Same deterministic NO answer, in the user's language (the Amharic template already existed). A non-ban Amharic question ("አካውንቴን እንዴት ማረጋገጥ እችላለሁ?") does not fire it.
- **Fault 3a — DELAYED vs ON-TRACK. Root cause:** `media_order_context` mapped ANY in-progress status (pending/in_progress/processing/partial) to "delayed" with no elapsed-time check, so a GIF while #1837 was merely in progress got the delay apology. **Fix:** "delayed" only when the order's elapsed time (from `created_at`, which GET /orders returns) is past its average time (`parse_average_time`), or it failed; otherwise the situation is the new "on_track". Unknown created_at or average time → "delayed" (the old behaviour — a delay apology is the safe reply when the bot genuinely cannot tell). `media_reply` maps a mood-less GIF with an on-track order to happy (celebration, never an apology); new `frustrated_on_track` and `neutral_on_track` reply groups for the frustrated/neutral moods. The existing delayed-order GIF test still passes (created_at 2026-11-13, avg 12 min → elapsed is days).
- **Fault 3b — WAVE READ AS THANKS. Root cause:** `_MEDIA_MOODS` mapped "👋" to "thanks", so the sticker got "You're welcome!". **Fix:** new "greeting" mood — 👋, 🙋, ✋ all greet ("Hello! 👋 ..."), en + am. 🙏 as a sticker is still thanks; bare 🙏 as TEXT starts a reactions order now (Fault 1) — a different signal.
- **BROADENED EMOJI AUDIT (William, 14 Nov 18:19 EAT — "He also misunderstood the emoji").** Audited every single-emoji path: bare 👀, 💔, 🤗, 🥰, 💯 (not a reaction emoji, no media mood, not a chat emoji) fell through to "I did not understand", and a bare reaction emoji while a Confirm card for ANOTHER service was pending did too. **Fix:** new `_is_bare_emoji(text)` (message is only emoji, 1+ code points, VS16/skin tones/joiners/keycaps stripped) + a catch-all in `handle_order_text` after the bare-emoji block: an emoji the reactions path does not claim, or any bare emoji while a card is pending, gets a situation-aware media reply — never "I did not understand", never touches the draft or the card. A bare reaction emoji while a non-reactions DRAFT is being built keeps the old behaviour (the flow re-asks for the missing piece). One existing test updated deliberately: `test_thanks_emoji_gets_a_warm_short_reply` → `test_wave_emoji_gets_a_greeting_reply` (👋 now greets).
- **BUILT + VERIFIED 14 Nov 18:2x EAT (714/714 tests: 695 baseline + 19 new TestRetestFixRound14Nov).** NOT pushed/deployed — parent verifies and pushes. Files changed: bot.py, test_bot_offline.py, TRAINING.md, HANDOVER.md.
- **Not yet verified live:** the whole round end-to-end after deploy — "10 🙏" → post-link ask, then bare link → Pray #1723 card qty 10; bare "🙏" → post-link ask; Amharic ban question with the bridge down → NO answer in Amharic; happy GIF with an on-track order → warm reply; 👋/🙋 sticker → greeting; bare 👀 → sensible reply, never "I did not understand". The live #1837 question (was it really on track at GIF time?) cannot be settled retroactively — it depends on its created_at vs its 4-minute average time, which the retest did not record.
- **DEEPSEEK-FIRST MEDIA REPLIES — BUILT + VERIFIED 14 Nov 18:51 EAT (729/729 tests: 714 baseline + 16 new TestDeepSeekFirstMediaReplies − 1 deleted rotation test).** NOT pushed/deployed — parent verifies and pushes. **Design (William, 14 Nov 18:43 EAT):** the bot still fails on GIFs/emojis — new design: DeepSeek replies FIRST, naturally, to the GIF/sticker/emoji itself; THEN the system's memory is added ONLY if the order is actually delayed/failed (one short line with the real order). If there is no delay — even if the user is frustrated right after placing an order — the reply is just the normal DeepSeek reply and must NOT mention the order every time. **What changed:** `media_reply` now calls new `media_ai_reply(kind, emoji, lang)` (prompt = media kind + emoji + language only; no order info ever reaches the AI — a test asserts the prompt and system prompt contain no order id; small dedicated system prompt, same pattern as `answer_general_question`; `strip_moralizing` applied), then appends new `delay_line(ctx, lang)` only when `media_order_context` says delayed/failed (en: "By the way — your order #1836 (Instagram Followers) is still pending, usually about 12 minutes." / failed: "…didn't go through."; am equivalents starting "በነገራችን ላይ"). `on_animation` passes kind="gif", `on_sticker` kind="sticker", bare-emoji text keeps kind="emoji". **Deleted:** the canned rotating replies — `_MEDIA_REPLIES`, `MEDIA_REPLY_COUNT`, `media_reply_text` (the rotation test was deleted deliberately; the delay logic is still covered by `delay_line` unit tests + `_order_is_delayed` tests). `_MEDIA_MOODS`/`media_mood` stay: they drive `_bare_non_reaction_emoji` and the AI-down fallback. **Fallback:** when every provider fails, one short mood-aware deterministic line (`_media_ai_fallback`: wave greets, frustration is heard, else a warm line) — never silent; the delay line is still appended when owed. **Tests updated deliberately:** the happy-GIF-after-completed test now asserts NO order id in the reply; the frustrated-sticker-no-order test asserts the reply IS the AI text; the pending-card test asserts "Confirm" is NOT in the media reply; the wave/raised-hand/pray sticker tests now mock the AI greeting/thanks. **Root cause it fixes:** the old path always mentioned the order whenever one was in progress (and picked the mood from the situation, so a cheers GIF during an on-track order got the delay apology). **Not yet verified live:** GIF/sticker/emoji with a delayed order → AI reply + delay line; GIF with an on-track order → AI reply only, no order mention; GIF with no orders → AI reply only; 👋 sticker → AI greeting; Amharic GIF + delayed order → Amharic reply + Amharic delay line.

## 14 Nov 19:xx EAT — Word quantities + platform from the link (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **LIVE FAILURES (William, 14 Nov 18:52–18:55 EAT, screenshots):** "I want like https://t.me/godofpanelcom/768 hundred views for this can you do this" AND "I want like 100 views for this https://t.me/godofpanelcom/768" (both with the GodofPanel TikTok-notice post attached/forwarded) both got "I could not find a service for that platform. Please try another platform." — even the digit-quantity version failed. **William's rules:** quantities in word form must work like digits; the platform comes from the LINK itself (t.me → Telegram, facebook.com → Facebook, instagram.com → Instagram, tiktok.com → TikTok, youtube.com → YouTube), ignoring forwarded text about other platforms; and "t.me is not only telegram post, it could also be telegram channel."
- **Fault 1 — QUANTITY IN WORD FORM NOT UNDERSTOOD. Root cause (reproduced offline, /tmp/repro_words_link.py):** `quantity_from_text` read digits only — "hundred views" gave None, so MSG1 asked "How many?" instead of showing the card. **Fix:** new `parse_number_words` + `_number_word_values`/`_number_phrase_value` (bot.py, next to `quantity_from_text`): ones zero–nineteen, tens twenty–ninety, scales hundred/hundreds=100, thousand/thousands=1000, million/millions=1000000; "a"/"an" counts as 1 next to a number word ("a hundred" = 100), "and" skipped between number words ("one hundred and fifty" = 150); a phrase is a maximal run of number words. Only a phrase that is a valid quantity (multiple of 10, 10..1,000,000) counts — "one" is None, "twenty one" is None, "three million" is None. Hooked into `quantity_from_text` (digits first, then word fallback — existing digit behaviour unchanged), `quantity_before_metric` (word-phrase variant: phrase, then up to 2 filler words, then the metric, mirroring the digit regex — "hundred tiktok views" = 100), `bare_quantity` ("one thousand please" = 1000, "hundred" = 100 — covers quantity answers), `local_intent` (its digit loop replaced by `quantity_from_text`), and `_quantity_grounded_in_text` (a word phrase equal to the wanted value grounds it). Deliberately NO blanket `quantity_from_text` fallback in the merge — it would resurrect the round-7 "my balance is 100" bug (a stray number becoming the quantity). "1k"/"2.5k" already worked via `normalize_text` — verified, not changed. **Amharic number words: English only** — grep found no Amharic quantity handling anywhere in bot.py (no መቶ/ሺህ/አንድ/ሁለት/አስር), so none was added; Amharic orders go through the translate bridge, which turns "አንድ ሺህ" into "one thousand" before the quantity is read.
- **Fault 2a — FILLER "LIKE" READ AS THE METRIC. Root cause (reproduced offline):** `kind_from_text` scanned words in order and "like" (filler in "I want, like, 100 views") was the first metric word, before "views" — so `detect_platform_metric` returned ('telegram', 'likes'), and with Telegram reactions services' name-kinds {reactions, views, likes} not a subset of hint-kinds {likes}, the pick pool was empty → "could not find a service". **Fix:** in `kind_from_text`, skip "like"/"likes" when a quantity follows within the next 3 tokens (digits or a number word) — "I want like 100 views" → "views", "like 100 likes" → still "likes" (the later word), "please hundreds like for this" → "likes" (no quantity after "like"), "likes for my video" → "likes". The comment-likes check ("comment"+"like") runs first and is unaffected. The scan now strips URLs first (their words "t"/"me"/"com" are not the user's words — in MSG1 the URL sat between "like" and "hundred" and pushed the quantity out of the 3-token window) and keeps digit tokens in the scan so the lookahead can see "100".
- **Fault 2b — PLATFORM DID NOT COME FROM THE LINK. Root cause (reproduced offline):** `platform_from_link("https://t.me/godofpanelcom/768")` already returned "telegram" and `local_intent` was already link-first — but the merge in `handle_order_text` took `merged["platform"]` only from the AI intent or the stored draft (the link was used only in the mismatch guard), and `detect_platform_metric` was words-first, then link. So the AI's wrong platform ("tiktok" from the forwarded TikTok notice, or "telegram likes" from the filler "like") survived. **Fix:** (a) `detect_platform_metric` is now links-first, then words (its only caller is `build_classify_prompt`); (b) in the merge, right after the "link always wins" block: when the merged link has a recognisable domain, `merged["platform"]` is set from it and any platform word in the hint is replaced with the link platform — UNLESS the user explicitly typed a different platform word in the message (new `_explicit_platform_word`, scanning words with URLs stripped via the `https?://\S+|t\.me/\S+` regex, using `_PLATFORM_WORDS`); in that explicit case the AI/stored platform is kept so the existing `platform_mismatch` guard still fires ("YouTube subscribers" + TikTok link → "That link is tiktok, but you asked for youtube").
- **Fault 3 — T.ME CHANNEL LINK NOT RECOGNISED AS A POST LINK. Root cause:** `link_content_kind` already returned "profile" for a channel link, but nothing in the flow distinguished a channel link from a post link for a post-needing metric — the pick then failed on the channel link. **Fix:** after the TikTok link-kind block in `handle_order_text`, when the link is Telegram + `link_content_kind` == "profile" (channel) + the metric needs a post (new `_needs_post_link`: `metric_content_kind(hint) == "video"` or "reaction" in hint — views, likes, comments, shares, reactions), the link is dropped from PARTIAL (platform/hint/quantity kept) and the bot asks for the specific post link with the new `channel_link_needs_post` template (en: "That link is a channel — for {metric} I need the link of the specific post (it looks like t.me/channelname/123). Please send the post link."; am equivalent), placed next to the link-kind templates. Followers/members/subscribers take a channel link as-is — no check. The follow-up post link alone completes the order (the draft keeps the quantity).
- **BUILT + VERIFIED 14 Nov 19:xx EAT (749/749 tests: 729 baseline + 20 new TestWordQuantitiesAndLinkPlatform).** NOT pushed/deployed — parent verifies and pushes. Files changed: bot.py, test_bot_offline.py, TRAINING.md, HANDOVER.md. New fixture `LinkPlatformPaxyo(FakePaxyo)` — one service per (platform, metric) pair from the real 12 Nov catalogue rows: #6139 Telegram Post Views (0.42, 16 min), #5001 Facebook Post Likes (29.40, 30 min), #1665 TikTok Video Views (18.90, 4 min), #1001 Instagram Followers (42.00, 12 min), #7001 YouTube Video Views (42.00, 30 min), #2001 Telegram Channel Members (42.00, 60 min) — so a wrong platform or metric shows up as the wrong service id. E2E tests mock a WRONG AI intent on purpose (e.g. platform="tiktok" for a t.me link, "telegram likes" for a views message) to prove the deterministic code corrects it. **Test updated deliberately:** `test_bare_link_with_no_context_asks_platform` → `test_bare_link_with_no_context_keeps_platform_from_link` — a bare instagram.com link now keeps Instagram from the link and asks "How many?", never "Which platform?" (the owner's new rule supersedes the old expectation). **Repro note:** the /tmp repro script's Facebook case still shows "could not find a service" — that is the repro fixture's catalogue gap (`RealReactionsPaxyo` has only Facebook *Reactions* services, no Facebook Post Likes service), not a code failure: the new `test_facebook_post_link_hundreds_like` (fixture has #5001) passes. The repro's Telegram cases now show Confirm cards (the fixture picks #216 "Telegram Bot Start" because it has no Telegram Post Views service; the new fixture picks #6139).
- **Not yet verified live:** the two exact live messages → Telegram Post Views #6139 card qty 100; Facebook post link + "hundreds like" → Facebook likes card qty 100; t.me channel link + "100 views" → post-link ask, then the post link → #6139 card; a word-quantity answer ("one thousand") after "How many?" → card qty 1000.
- **Next round (APPROVED by William 19:07 EAT, "Ok"):** the smart-loop round, AFTER this round lands — order: failure log/golden dataset + few-shot examples + prompt restructure (static first, dynamic last) + Whisper domain prompt + session store + strict-mode extraction; adversarial harness later; vector DB skipped for now; no fine-tuning.

## 14 Nov 19:xx EAT — Fix round: bare link loses the reaction + bare Cancel free-chat (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **LIVE RETEST 19:15–19:30 EAT (deploy dep-dbs8h167bikc73etia6g, commit 50562b4) — 6 OF 7 PASS.** Word quantities ("hundred"→100, "one thousand"→1000, "hundreds"→100), filler-"like", channel-link ask and Facebook likes all pass. NOT a bug: live Telegram Post Views picks #1872 (36.75 ETB/1000, 3 min) over #6139 (1.05) — fastest-first by design; Facebook picks #5539 (149.60, 1 min), a real Post Likes service. **FAIL (check 6):** bare "10 🙏" → post-link ask ✓, then the bare link → "How many? (for example 1000)" instead of the Pray card; answering "10" built a Telegram Post Views #1872 card qty 10. The 🙏 reaction context was lost across the link/quantity steps — it WORKED at 19:08 EAT on commit 272609a. **Second fault:** the final "Cancel" with nothing pending got a DeepSeek free-chat continuation ("These services deliver gradually and are widely used, but no one can guarantee zero risk — and followers come from a global network…") instead of "Nothing to cancel." — seen twice now (19:10 and 19:29 EAT). William approved the fix ("Yes, fix these two faults", 19:37 EAT).
- **Fault 1 — BARE LINK LOSES THE REACTION. Root cause (reproduced offline, /tmp/repro_reaction_loss.py):** the AI guesses "telegram views" for a bare t.me post link. In the merge, `merged["service_hint"]` took the AI's hint ("telegram views") over the stored reactions draft, and the metric-change check then saw views ≠ reactions and reset the whole draft (quantity 10 included) — so the flow asked "How many?" and the answer built a views card. The existing test only covered the bare link with an "unclear" intent, which passes; the failure needs the AI's wrong hint. **Fix:** a bare-link slot-fill guard at the top of the merge, before the platform-mismatch reset: when a stored draft exists AND the message is only a link AND `kind_from_text(text)` is None (URLs stripped, so a bare link names no metric) AND no explicit platform word was typed AND the link's platform matches the draft's (or has none, like a @handle), the AI's guessed `platform` and `service_hint` are dropped for the turn (`intent = dict(intent)` first, so the caller's dict is untouched) — the stored draft's metric, emoji and quantity survive, and the link fills in as usual below. A message WITH words naming another metric still goes through the normal metric-change logic, and a bare link for a different platform still starts a fresh order (the guard's platform check fails, so the reset fires). The fastest-pick selection is untouched: #1872 over #6139 is by design.
- **Fault 2 — BARE CANCEL GETS A FREE-CHAT CONTINUATION. Root cause (reproduced offline, /tmp/repro_cancel.py):** "Cancel" is only a cancel when the classifier says so, and with nothing pending the AI returns off_topic (or unclear) — off_topic calls `answer_general_question`, which continued the ban thread with "no one can guarantee zero risk", contradicting the standing ban NO answer; unclear got "I did not understand". `conversation_reply` does not swallow it (`is_free_chat("cancel")` is False) and `local_intent`'s `"cancel" in s` only runs as a Gemini-failure fallback. **Fix:** (a) new `is_cancel_request(text)` (next to `is_usual_request`): exact match on "cancel", "cancel it/this/that/my order/the order", "never mind", "forget it", bare "stop" (+ "stop it/this/the order/my order"), "please cancel…" prefix, and "don't want to"/"don't want it" substrings (the owner's "What if I don't want to"). NOT matched: questions about cancelling ("can I cancel my order after it starts?"), "cancel my schedule" / "cancel the repeating order" (those stay with the classifier → stop_schedule), "how do I cancel an order?". (b) In `handle_order_text`, right after the usual-request block and before the classifier: `if intent is None and is_cancel_request(text): intent = _empty_intent("cancel")` — so the existing cancel branch always runs, whatever the AI would have said. The terms answer still runs first (it sits before the cancel branch), so "can I cancel after it starts?" still gets "can't be canceled". (c) New template `nothing_to_cancel` (en: "Nothing to cancel."; am: "ምንም የሚሰርዝ ነገር የለም።"). (d) The cancel branch's final fallthrough (no pending order, no recurring/deposit/batch, no schedules) now checks whether anything was actually being built (`PARTIAL` or `RECURRING_DRAFT`/`DEPOSIT_DRAFT`/`SPLIT_DRAFT` non-empty, captured BEFORE `clear_order_state`) and replies "Order cancelled." only then — otherwise "Nothing to cancel.". The pending-card, recurring, deposit and batch branches are unchanged.
- **BUILT + VERIFIED 14 Nov 19:xx EAT (767/767 tests: 749 baseline + 18 new TestBareLinkReactionAndCancelFix).** NOT pushed/deployed — parent verifies and pushes. Files changed: bot.py, test_bot_offline.py, TRAINING.md, HANDOVER.md. New tests cover the exact live flows: bare link with a "telegram views" hint → #1723 card qty 10 with no "How many?" re-ask; same with a "telegram post views" hint; bare "🙏" → link → "How many?" → "10" → #1723 card; "10 ❤️" → #6537, "10 🔥" → #6540, "10 👍" → #6541 (exact emoji kept through the bare link); channel link after a reactions draft → post-link ask, then the post link → #1723 card; a link WITH words ("1000 views for this …") still changes the metric → #6139 card (LinkPlatformPaxyo fixture); a bare TikTok link after a reactions draft starts a fresh order ("How many?", no reaction_emoji in PARTIAL); Cancel with nothing pending → "Nothing to cancel." for cancel/off_topic/unclear intents and with the classifier never called (extract_intent raises if invoked); Cancel with a draft or a card pending → "Order cancelled."; "can I cancel after it starts?" → terms answer. **Test updated deliberately:** `test_cancel_without_pending_says_cancelled` → `test_cancel_without_pending_says_nothing_to_cancel` — "What if I don't want to" with nothing pending now expects "Nothing to cancel." (the owner's new rule supersedes the old expectation).
- **Not yet verified live:** "10 🙏" → post-link ask → bare link → Pray #1723 card qty 10 with no quantity re-ask; bare "🙏" → link → "How many?" → "10" → #1723 card; bare "Cancel" with nothing pending → "Nothing to cancel." (and never the "zero risk" continuation); Cancel with a draft/card pending → "Order cancelled."

- **William's instruction, 19:49 EAT:** "Not only this emoji 🙏 — include many emoji and let the bot recognise them." The bare-reaction order flow must recognise MANY emojis, not just 🙏.
- **Audit (catalogue snapshot 12 Nov 2026, 7,175 services):** Telegram single-emoji reactions services — 65xx series at rate 0.013 (5.46 ETB/1000) and 17xx/20xx at 0.03/0.039/0.0686/0.10 (12.60/16.38/28.81/42.00); Facebook generic single-emoji reactions (the rest of the 404 FB reactions services are geo-targeted variants — USA/Latin America/Germany/etc. — excluded). No exact service anywhere: 😄 🥳 🤯 🥺 🙌 👏 😊. 👀 Eyes #6568 exists but 👀 stays the "looking" media emoji per the task, and 😤 stays frustrated media.
- **What changed (bot.py):** `EMOJI_MEANINGS` + `REACTION_EMOJI` + `REACTION_FAMILY_WORDS` extended to the full map (84 REACTION_EMOJI members; every member has a family entry — verified by script — because a missing entry makes `_reactions_pool` fall back to the whole pool and silently sell a mix). `emoji_meanings` now joins ZWJ sequences, so ❤️‍🔥 is ONE emoji (heart-on-fire, #6536), never ❤️ plus 🔥, and looks the full emoji up before its base char (a joined sequence with no meaning of its own contributes nothing — its parts are never read as separate emoji). `_service_in_reaction_family` compares emoji ignoring the U+FE0F variation selector (a bare ❤ matches the ❤️ service, a bare ⚡ the ⚡️ service) and its cross-family guard skips VS twins, so a twin's own word never blocks the match. `conversation_reply` no longer swallows a message that is exactly one reaction emoji as free chat — without this, 😂/🤣/😄/😊 (also in `_CHAT_EMOJI`) never reached the reactions branch; "haha 😂" is still free chat. Three stale comments/docstrings updated deliberately (`_bare_non_reaction_emoji`, `_is_bare_emoji`, the bare-emoji catch-all — 🎉/💔/🤗 are reactions now, 👀/😤 are the non-reaction examples). **The pick is unchanged:** cheapest-first within one exact emoji (owner decision, 14 Nov 2026, in `pick_services`' docstring) — the task said "fastest, never cheapest" but also "do NOT change the fastest-pick service selection"; in this fixture cheapest is also fastest, so the tests cannot tell them apart. **Open decision for the parent:** cheapest-within-emoji (current) vs fastest-within-emoji. Prices unchanged (provider rate ×420 ETB). Media-reply design untouched (DeepSeek first, delay line only when actually delayed). Cancel behaviour untouched.
