## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Admin chat bot: new bot, live questions, targeted broadcasts, ticker changes (built, verified offline, NOT pushed/deployed/installed)
- **Decision (William, 15 Nov 07:12 EAT): this is a BRAND-NEW bot, NOT @paxadmin_bot** (Abel's / already taken). Code reads its token from env `ADMIN_BOT_TOKEN`, never hardcoded; the token comes later when William or Abel creates the bot in BotFather. Polling, not webhook (no conflict with @paxorder_bot — different bot/token). New files only: `admin_bot.py`, `test_admin_bot_offline.py` (+ `server/api-admin-broadcasts.php`, `server/api-admin-settings.php`, extended `server/api-admin-stats.php`). `bot.py` / `test_bot_offline.py` untouched by this workstream.
- **What it does (William only, English only):** (1) LIVE QUESTIONS — "how much did we make today?", "how many users in the last 7 days?", orders / deposits / top service — numbers from GET /admin/stats, the same endpoint the digest uses; 404 → honest "not connected yet", never invented numbers. (2) TARGETED BROADCASTS — "send this to users who never deposited: <message>" → bot counts via GET /broadcasts/audience, shows count + exact message, William taps Confirm, only then the bot sends sequentially via its own token and reports delivered/failed. Segments: never_deposited, deposited, service (by id, or by name resolved via the catalogue), all. (3) SETTINGS — "change ticker to ..." → preview, Confirm, then POST /admin/settings. Ticker first; endpoint generic by key.
- **Audience contract (authoritative — the admin-panel targeted-broadcasts feature reuses it):** GET /broadcasts/audience?segment=<all|never_deposited|deposited|service>&service_id=<id> → {segment, service_id, count, telegram_ids}, DISTINCT ids (ordered twice counts once; never-ordered never included). Only COMPLETED deposits count as "deposited". Audience resolution is server-side; SENDING is bot-side only. Verified: deposits.user_id IS the telegram id (api-deposits.php INSERT binding); auth.tg_id is the Telegram id.
- **Stats extension (same file, no competing endpoint):** GET /admin/stats?days=N (default 1, clamped 1..90, EAT calendar days, today included); response adds `from`, `days`, `deposits_count`, `deposits_etb` (completed deposits — money in, never added to revenue_etb). "Yesterday" alone is recognised but NOT supported (windows end today) — the bot says so honestly.
- **Settings endpoint:** POST /admin/settings {key, value} → {ok, key, value}; key ^[a-z0-9_]{1,64}$, value ≤2000 chars; UPDATE-then-INSERT. Settings-table shape NOT confirmed from local files — key/value columns discovered at runtime (key|name|setting_key, value|setting_value|setting); missing table/columns → plain 500. **TO VERIFY before go-live: the ticker key** — bot sends `ticker_text` (TICKER_SETTING_KEY in admin_bot.py); check what key the site/mini app actually reads and change that one constant if it differs.
- **Understanding:** deterministic regex parsing FIRST (stats questions, broadcast commands, ticker changes); AI only for free-form — DeepSeek first, then Gemini, reusing the order bot's existing keys; no key → short "I didn't understand — try ..." reply, never silence. The AI is never called for a broadcast or a setting change and its output can never trigger an action (test-proven with a raising mock).
- **Safety (never break):** ONLY William — allowlist from env ADMIN_TELEGRAM_IDS, empty by default = refuses everyone. EVERY change needs an explicit Confirm step (tap or typed "yes"); nothing sends/writes on a single message; a double Confirm runs once (pending entry cleared before executing). Money movement out of scope — never touches balances, orders, deposits or refunds. Never reveals provider names, buying costs, secrets, or "SMM panel". English only.
- **Verified offline: 71/71 tests** (test_admin_bot_offline.py, FakeAdminPaxyo/FakeMessage style): allowlist, stats answers incl. 404/deposits-missing/yesterday honesty, broadcast preview/Confirm/dedupe/failed-count/cancel/404/zero-audience, ticker preview/no-write-before-Confirm/write-after-Confirm, service-name resolution (exact/unique-substring/ambiguous→asks for ID), AI fallback + AI-never-called proofs, double-Confirm-once. **test_bot_offline.py: 967/967 OK** (untouched by this workstream). No PHP lint run here (no PHP in the sandbox) — `php -l` must run on the server at install.
- **Still needed:** BotFather token (→ Render ADMIN_BOT_TOKEN); a Render service for admin_bot.py (polling; env ADMIN_BOT_TOKEN, BOT_API_SECRET, ADMIN_TELEGRAM_IDS = William's id; AI keys optional, reusable from the order bot); server installs (re-splice updated api-admin-stats.php; splice api-admin-broadcasts.php + api-admin-settings.php; php -l; footer curl tests; verify ticker key + orders service column live). Until the endpoints are live, the bot answers "not connected yet" and sends/writes nothing.

## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Automatic stall rescue (feature 2) (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **What it does.** When an order the bot placed stalls (the existing is_order_stuck rule: elapsed ≥ 3× the claimed average time AND ≥ 15 minutes overdue; no average time → never stuck), the delivery watch now tries to rescue it once: cancel + refund it in full through the new server endpoint, then re-place the same order on the next-best service in the same category — no Confirm card (the user already confirmed the order; the rescue only carries it out on the next service, same fulfilment design as §1). At most two rescues per chain (#1 -> #2 -> #3); when the third also stalls the bot stops and says so honestly. A rescued order gets the rescue message, not the stuck warning, for that episode. Any failure (refund refused, no next service, replacement fails) falls back to the existing one stuck warning, and the rescue is never tried again for that order.
- **What changed — bot.py, client.** `PaxyoClient.cancel_refund(order_id, telegram_id)` — POST /orders/{id}/cancel-refund {telegram_id}, 404-tolerant like save_session/load_session (returns None, logs a warning — the endpoint is not live yet); any other error raises (409 already completed/cancelled or provider-refused, 502 provider did not confirm). The server contract is server/api-order-rescue.php: a repeat call returns the recorded result with duplicate:true and the same refunded_etb, never a second refund; the server NEVER places the replacement order.
- **What changed — bot.py, watch.** `MAX_STALL_RESCUES = 2`. `watch_order` entries now carry `total` (what the user paid), `rescue_count`, `rescue_attempted`, `failed_services` (service ids that already stalled in this chain); all three placement call sites pass the total. New `_next_rescue_service` (same platform + metric via `_platform_hint_of_service`, fastest-first via `pick_services` limit 3, excluding every service that already stalled in the chain — not just the latest one, or the second rescue would go back to the first failure) and `_attempt_stall_rescue` (finds the next service FIRST, so an order with no next service is never cancelled; then cancel_refund; then a balance check against the refunded balance; then `create_order` with a FRESH uuid.uuid4().hex idempotency key, never the original order's key; on success drops the old id, watches the new id with rescue_count+1 and the failed list extended, sets LAST_ORDER to the new id, and sends the rescue message). The stuck branch in `run_delivery_watch_cycle`: on the first stuck cycle, if rescues remain it attempts the rescue (marks `rescue_attempted` either way); on success the cycle moves on; on failure it falls through to the one stuck warning. When `rescue_count` is already 2 it sends the final `order_rescue_exhausted` message once instead and keeps watching, so a late delivery still gets its message. New templates `order_rescued` and `order_rescue_exhausted` (en + first-pass am; the watch sends English, like the other scheduler notices).
- **Exact user-visible behaviour.** Rescue: "⏳ Your order #1841 (TikTok Video Views ×1000) stalled, so I cancelled it and refunded 10.00 ETB to your balance. I've moved your order to the next service — TikTok Video Views — as order #1842. The new order costs 5.00 ETB (the refunded order was 10.00 ETB)." (figures are the live ones — refund from the server, new total from the server reply or the catalogue estimate.) Exhausted: "⏳ Your order #1843 (TikTok Video Views ×1000) is still not delivering — we already moved it to the next service twice and both stalled too, so we've stopped moving it. Your order is still in progress with the last service. If it stays stuck, talk to support: t.me/paxyo" — no refund is claimed for the third order, because it is still in progress, not refunded.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** FakePaxyo gained `cancel_refund` (seeded result; None by default — endpoint not live; `cancel_refund_error=<status>` raises the HTTP error), `cancel_refund_calls`, and `next_order_ids` (ids create_order returns, 1827 by default). New `TestStallRescue` (18 tests): cancel_refund client (success body/URL/header, duplicate passthrough, 404 → None, 409 raises); next-service pick (excludes all prior failures, None when all failed); watch_order rescue state; rescue happy path (refund + replacement on the next service, both totals, new id, old id dropped, chain carried forward, fresh 32-hex idempotency key); attempted once over three cycles; replacement itself watched (completes → delivery message); 409/None/502 → warning only, no retry; no-next-service → warning only, cancel never called; replacement failure → warning only; all-3-fail → final honest message, then silence; slow-but-within-time and no-average-time orders untouched. **No existing test was changed.**
- **BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 07:xx EAT (985/985 tests: 967 baseline + 18 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed.**
- **What to verify LIVE after the server endpoint is installed:** the curl test in server/api-order-rescue.php first (small test order, William's approval — it cancels a real order on purpose); then one real end-to-end stuck rescue, checking the message figures match the server refund and the new order's charged total, and that the replacement completes and gets its delivery message.
- **Open questions / caveats:** (a) refund-succeeded-but-replacement-failed still yields the generic stuck warning ("still being worked on") for an already-cancelled order — consider a dedicated "refunded but couldn't move it" message; (b) in-memory watch: a restart loses the chain state, so a previously-rescued order could be rescued again after a restart (the server's duplicate protection still prevents a second refund of the same order); (c) reactions orders: the rescue pick does not carry the original reaction emoji, so a rescued reactions order gets the generic reactions-pool pick; (d) if the server's refund reply ever omits refunded_etb, the bot falls back to the stored watch total, then to a catalogue estimate — verify the live reply carries refunded_etb.

## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Abandoned-order nudge (feature 4) (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **What it does.** A half-built NORMAL order draft (PARTIAL) whose owner goes quiet for ~30 minutes gets ONE nudge with a Resume button: "You left an order half-built — want to finish it? Tap below to pick up where you left off." Never more than once per draft; never while a Confirm card is open for that user (they are deciding, not abandoning); never after the draft was cancelled or the order completed (both drop PARTIAL); never for delayed or recurring drafts — those live in DELAYED_DRAFT / RECURRING_DRAFT, never in PARTIAL, so the cycle never sees them. The nudge only invites the user back — it never places, confirms, or changes an order.
- **What changed — bot.py.** New state `PARTIAL_ACTIVITY` (telegram_id → {"last_active" epoch, "nudged_at" epoch or None, "draft_key" fingerprint of platform/service_hint/link/quantity}) and `ABANDONED_NUDGE_MINUTES = 30`. `touch_partial_activity(user_id)` stamps the clock on every user message while a draft exists (top of `handle_order_text`, right after `restore_session` — quiet means no messages at all), on the first-order-nudge tap, and on the Resume tap; a draft with different content resets its own nudge allowance. `clear_order_state` clears the activity entry on cancel. `run_abandoned_nudge_cycle(app, now=None)` runs inside the existing 60-second `run_scheduler_cycle`, after the delivery watch: a draft quiet ≥30 min with no Confirm card open and no nudge yet gets the nudge in the user's language (LAST_LANG — one dict read; Tigrinya/Oromo fall back to English via `t()`), sent best-effort through `notify_user` (gained an optional `reply_markup`; failures logged, never raise into the cycle). A draft first seen with no activity entry gets its clock seeded at first sight, not nudged at once. New callback `resume_draft`: gone draft → honest "there's no half-built order to resume"; otherwise re-asks the next missing piece via the existing `_fallback_slot_question` (platform → link → quantity, same order as the normal flow — no second continuation path); a draft with all pieces present shows its Confirm card via `complete_order`. New templates `abandoned_nudge`, `abandoned_resume_button`, `resume_draft_gone` (en + first-pass am, unreviewed). Confirm flow untouched; no provider/source/cost wording.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** `FakeBot.send_message` accepts `reply_markup=None` and records markups in `sent_markups` (`sent` stays 2-tuples — no existing assertion moved). New `TestAbandonedNudge` (18 tests): nudge fires once after ~30 min for link-no-quantity and quantity-no-link drafts; not before 30 min; not twice even an hour later; not with a Confirm card open; not after cancel (real cancel flow) or after order completion (real Confirm-tap flow); not for delayed/recurring drafts; a fresh message resets the quiet timer; resume re-asks quantity/link/platform in order; resume with a complete draft shows the Confirm card; resume with a gone draft answers honestly; Amharic nudge + resume via LAST_LANG; nudge runs inside `run_scheduler_cycle`. **No existing test was changed.**
- **BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 07:xx EAT (1003/1003 tests: 985 baseline + 18 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed.**
- **RESTART LIMIT (investigated, not built around).** PARTIAL and PARTIAL_ACTIVITY are in-memory only. `restore_session` runs only from `handle_order_text`, once per user per process — the scheduler never calls it. So after a restart/redeploy, the scheduler cannot nudge a draft whose owner has not messaged since the restart: the draft sits in `bot_sessions` but PARTIAL is empty until their next message. Once they message, the draft is restored, the clock stamps at that message, and the nudge can fire 30 min later if they go quiet again. PARTIAL_ACTIVITY itself is never persisted (the session payload saves only history/draft/lang), so a restored draft's original last-activity time is lost. Same free-Render sleep caveat as the delivery watch: a nudge due while the process sleeps arrives on the next wake. Fixing this properly needs a server-side "drafts gone quiet" query — out of scope for this feature, no endpoint built.
- **Open questions:** (a) should the nudge name what's in the draft (e.g. "1000 TikTok views")? Currently generic per the spec wording. (b) Should a nudged-then-ignored draft ever get a second nudge after a much longer silence (e.g. 24h)? Currently never — once per draft. (c) Amharic templates are first-pass, unreviewed. (d) The resume-complete case (all pieces present, e.g. metric question pending) shows the Confirm card — worth a live feel-check after deploy.
- **What to verify LIVE after deploy:** build a draft (e.g. "1000 tiktok views" + link, no quantity), wait ~30 min without messaging, confirm exactly one nudge arrives with the Resume button; tap it and confirm it asks "How many?"; confirm no second nudge arrives; confirm no nudge arrives for a draft with a Confirm card open, or after cancelling.

## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Per-user personality (feature 5), bot side (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **What it does.** The bot remembers each user: their usual order (platform, metric, quantity, service) and their tone (brief / chatty / formal) — learned only from their own messages and placed orders, never invented. Tone shapes the prose order confirmation; the usuals feed the "same as usual" fallback when there is no recent order. Prices, the Confirm flow and order logic are untouched; language is unchanged (lang from bot_sessions, never duplicated into traits).
- **What changed — bot.py, client.** `PaxyoClient.save_traits` (POST /user-traits), `load_traits` (GET /user-traits), `clear_traits` (POST /user-traits/clear) — all 404-tolerant like save_session/load_session (endpoint not live yet → None + warning, never raises into a chat). Saves send ONLY the traits that changed (the server merges; a null clears one field). The server contract is server/api-user-traits.php (Option B: separate bot_user_traits table — a traits save can never touch bot_sessions and vice versa).
- **What changed — bot.py, learning.** State: `USER_TRAITS` mirror (bounded to 500 users, oldest evicted), `_USUAL_COUNTS`, `_TONE_SIGNALS`, `_TRAITS_FROM_SERVER`, `_LAST_TRAITS_SAVE` (5s throttle, same shape as session saves). `restore_session` loads traits alongside the session via `_restore_traits`. `learn_from_placed_order` hooks `place_pending_order` (normal Confirm, order-now, text-"yes") and `place_batch`: the mirror always keeps the most recent placed order, but it only counts as the "usual" for defaults — and only gets saved — once the same platform+metric has been placed TWICE (`USUAL_MIN_PLACEMENTS = 2`); one placement is a one-off. Metrics are slugified for the server ("comment likes" → "comment_likes"). `observe_tone` runs on every non-button-tap user message: one vote per message, at most one signal (formal first: "please"/"kindly"/"dear"/"እባክዎ"; then ≥12 words chatty; then 1–3 words brief; bare numbers/links/emoji carry no vote). Tone is set only after 3 votes for the same tone, strictly leading the others; once set, a different tone replaces it only when its votes exceed the current tone's by 3 (`_TONE_FLIP_MARGIN = 3`) — one contrary message never flips it. "Forget what you know about me" (and variants; negations like "don't forget me" do NOT match) wipes the server row via clear_traits plus the mirror and counters; the session (history/draft/language) is untouched.
- **What changed — bot.py, use.** `tone_order_summary` shapes the order confirmation in the reply helpers, NOT the AI prompt: brief → summary alone (no humour line, no hint message); chatty → summary + one warm line ("Thanks so much — enjoy the boost! 🎉"); formal → polite opening ("Thank you for your order."). Applied in `send_order_result` and the Confirm-tap path in `on_callback`; no learned tone → replies byte-identical to before. `handle_usual` falls back to `_usual_fallback` when there is no recent order on the server: pre-fills the draft with the stored usual platform/metric/quantity, pins the usual service via a new one-shot `USUAL_SERVICE` dict (same semantics as the `NUDGE_SERVICE` pin in `complete_order`), and asks for the link — the user still confirms before anything is placed. New templates `tone_chatty_order`, `tone_formal_order`, `traits_forgotten`, `usual_from_memory`, `usual_from_memory_no_qty` (en + first-pass am, unreviewed).
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** `FakePaxyo` gained `traits=` seeding plus `save_traits`/`load_traits`/`clear_traits` mirrors (merge semantics like the server) and `traits_saved`/`traits_loads`/`traits_cleared` records. New `TestTraitsApiClient` (7 tests: save/load/clear request shape + 404 → None, load null → None) and `TestPerUserPersonality` (24 tests: first order updates the mirror but is not yet a usual and saves nothing; second same order establishes it and saves only the four usual fields; a different metric does not establish; comment-likes slugifies; tone unset before 3 observations, then sets; one contrary message (3-vs-3) does not flip, 6-vs-3 does; forget clears server + mirror but not history/draft; "don't forget me" does not clear; brief/chatty/formal confirmations; no-traits user gets default replies unchanged; prices/Confirm untouched by traits; usual fallback after a restart with empty memory pins the usual service on the Confirm card; a single order does not drive the fallback; restore loads from the server, and with nothing stored is a no-op). **Existing tests: no assertion changed.** Four tests failed on first run from state pollution by the new module-level stores (tone votes / usuals accumulating for user 1 across tests); fix, per codebase convention: the traits stores are now cleared in every existing setUp/tearDown that already clears module state (54 individual sites + 30 tuple-loop sites). The suite was green again after that, before the new tests were added.
- **BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 07:xx EAT (1034/1034 tests: 1003 baseline + 31 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed.**
- **Server-side install steps (parent runs these on paxyo.et, DirectAdmin Terminal — same shape as the round-1 install):** 1. Upload `server/api-user-traits.php` to `public_html/api/`. 2. `php -l api-user-traits.php` — must pass. 3. Splice into `public_html/api/bot.php` BEFORE the 404 fallback: the dispatch lines from the bottom of the file (`user-traits` POST/GET, `user-traits/clear` POST), copying the `bot_rate_limit(...)` call from the orders handler onto them. 4. Append the file's code WITHOUT its first `<?php` line to the end of bot.php. 5. `php -l api/bot.php` — must pass before anything goes live. 6. Live curl test from the bottom of the file (load → null, save two traits, load back, merge a third, clear one field with null, invalid value → 400, unknown field dropped, session row untouched, clear whole row, load → null), then DELETE the test row from bot_user_traits and confirm it is gone. Only after the endpoints answer 200 should the bot deploy go out — until then the bot works from memory (404-tolerant) and nothing breaks.
- **What to verify LIVE after deploy:** (a) place the same service twice → "same as usual" uses it; place it once → "same as usual" still says no usual yet; (b) after a restart, "same as usual" with no recent order still offers the stored usual and the Confirm card pins that service; (c) three short messages → shorter confirmations, three long ones → the warm line, three "please" messages → the polite form; (d) "forget what you know about me" clears it and the next "same as usual" says no usual yet; (e) prices and the Confirm card are unchanged throughout.
- **Open questions / caveats:** (a) recurring schedules, delayed orders and stall-rescue replacements place orders without going through place_pending_order/place_batch, so they don't teach the usuals — recurring/delayed are the user's own choices and arguably should count; rescue replacements are system-driven and arguably should not. (b) A traits save dropped by the 5s throttle is only re-sent on the next learning event. (c) The usual counts live in memory only; a server-loaded mirror counts as established without re-counting. (d) Amharic templates are first-pass, unreviewed. (e) Traits are per-user private — never mentioned to anyone but the user themself, never another user's traits.

## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Service types: every type in the catalogue (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **What it does.** The bot now knows all 221 platform+metric types across 30 platforms (was 6 platforms, 10 metric kinds). Cheapest questions: 138 → 218 right, 0 wrong, 0 not-recognised (was 54); orders: 172 → 218 right, 0 wrong. Live-stream services are never sold as plain views ("cheapest youtube views" now quotes plain-views #2494 at 125.71 ETB, not live-stream #1928 at 54.60). "Twitter" and "x" are the same platform on both sides of the price gate. Types with genuinely no service answer honestly instead of guessing: Telegram followers ("doesn't have followers — order members instead"), channel joins, YouTube engagement package (Facebook has one), Shopee plain views (live viewers only), YouTube auto views (no subscription exists — plain views are closest), FB 🙏 / IG ❤️ reactions (no such service; the Positive mix is the closest). Reaction word forms ("pray"/"heart reactions") behave exactly like the emoji forms. SYSTEM_PROMPT lists all 30 platforms and the full metric vocabulary; the deterministic detectors remain the safety net if the AI drops a new type.
- **What changed — bot.py.** All 30 platforms in `_PLATFORMS` / `_PLATFORM_WORDS` / `platform_from_link` ("max" deliberately NOT a platform word — MAX private-channel services are reached via "private channel" phrasing or service ID). All metric kinds in `_METRICS`, `_MULTIWORD_KINDS`, `kind_from_text`, `_name_kinds` (saves, plays, live viewers, story views/likes/comments/poll votes, reposts, retweets, reshares, quotes, bookmarks, statistics, mentions, impressions, reach, listeners, downloads, coins, tokens, wishes, watchlist, emotions, traffic, reviews, tweets, connections, endorsements, poll votes, profile visits, comment likes/replies/reactions, bot start, channel boost, server boost, friend requests, direct messages, growth/engagement package, auto likes/views/comments/reactions, premium/online members, and more). Plain "views" excludes live-stream services; "live viewers" is its own kind. Whole-word hint matching ("views" ≠ "reviews", "tweets" ≠ "retweets", "x" ≠ "Max"); plural bug generalised to all hint words; descriptor stripping (`[Real Views]`, `Source: …`, `Views/Source From Followers`, `Free Views`); "tweet" is a product only when it IS the product; reshare ≡ shares; bundles count under EACH component kind. Twitter/X gate fixed on both sides (`is_cheapest_question` and `detect_platform_metric` now share `_PLATFORM_WORDS`); all other `_PLATFORMS` word-scans replaced with a `_platform_from_words` helper. New `no_service_reply()` + 6 translation strings (en/am), wired into `price_answer_text` and all six order-flow no-service sites. `_name_kinds` is now `lru_cache`d (pure function of name+category) — a production win, since kinds were recomputed for every service on every message. SYSTEM_PROMPT / few-shot extended (all 30 platforms + grouped metric vocabulary + "never normalise live viewers/story views/comment likes to a shorter word" + 6 new examples). Amharic `_AMHARIC_METRICS` extended where a natural word exists; kinds with no clean Amharic word stay English-only. Blacklist 5379/5834 verified excluded (pinned by a test). Confirm flow untouched; prices always from the catalogue.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** New `TestServiceTypes` (18 tests): table-driven cheapest-question test over all 221 inventory rows (exact-id, with 4 documented corrections) and order test over all rows (pick in the right pool); detection tests for new platforms; bare-cheapest gate tests; end-to-end order tests for spotify plays (#8161, 37.80), twitch live viewers (#1804, 535.50), whatsapp poll votes (#8773, 362.88), snapchat views bundle (#8765, 1,600.20), telegram followers honest-no-service, youtube views excludes live streams (#1943, 151.20), twitter retweets via both "twitter" and "x" (#3980, 412.99); corrected-price tests (youtube views #2494, telegram views #6139, tiktok live viewers #3769, youtube auto views honest); no-service reply tests; reaction word-form tests (FB ❤️ → #5105, TG 🙏 → #1723, FB 🙏 → none); blacklist test. Fixture built from the snapshot (real names/rates, min/max included). Suite now takes ~81s (was ~9s) because the two table-driven tests scan the 7,175-service snapshot 219×2 times — the price of pinning every type. **Existing tests changed deliberately (4):** "snapchat" → "myspace" in the two unknown-platform tests (snapchat is now known); SYSTEM_PROMPT caps raised 9700 → 11500 chars and 2100 → 2400 tokens; few-shot block 22 → 28 lines, 1200 → 1700 chars (6 new examples). No existing test pinned the old live-stream YouTube price — none needed updating.
- **New file:** `verdict-service-types.py` — the verdict harness (before/after evidence), kept in the workspace. Inventory at `service-types-inventory.md`; snapshot `godofpanel-live-2026-11-15.json` (7,175 services, 15 Nov 07:03 EAT).

The one NONE is YouTube auto views — by design: no true auto-views subscription exists in the snapshot (the 5 "auto" services are Lifetime Auto / Auto Replace refill wordings, not a subscription); the bot answers honestly instead of guessing. Four §2 price differences are corrections, not failures (all pool-RIGHT): Telegram views #6139 1.05 (inventory's #6534 is "Reactions + Free Views"); TikTok live viewers #3769 176.40 (inventory's #4332 is a live-stream LIKES service); Kick viewers #7517 29.40 (inventory's #4072 is a 1-day subscription series; bare "viewers" = live viewers); YouTube viewers #1774 18.90 (inventory marks the row AMBIG; bare "viewers" = live viewers).

## 15 Nov 07:xx EAT — Targeted broadcasts, admin panel (built, verified offline, NOT installed)
- **What it is:** audience targeting for the admin panel's Broadcasting section (px-4650dbff). The existing Broadcasting (scheduling, send history, delivery control, deleting a sent message, photo attach) is KEPT untouched — the new part is ONLY who the broadcast goes to. Flow: pick segment (+ service) -> live count shown -> admin confirms -> send is recorded.
- **New files only:** `server/admin-targeted-broadcasts.php` (764 lines, admin page for public_html/px-4650dbff/) + `test_targeted_broadcasts_offline.py` (26 tests, all passing). `bot.py` / `test_bot_offline.py` / HANDOVER.md untouched; no existing server file edited.
- **Audience contract REUSED, not reinvented:** GET /broadcasts/audience?segment=<all|never_deposited|deposited|service>&service_id=<id> (server/api-admin-broadcasts.php — the authority). DISTINCT ids (ordered twice counts once; never-ordered never included); only COMPLETED deposits count as "deposited". NO patch to api-admin-broadcasts.php needed — all four segments already exist; the service picker resolves names to ids via GET /api/bot/services (server-side, 1h cache).
- **Count before send, count only:** the page calls the audience endpoint server-side with X-Bot-Secret (from config_secret.php, never to the browser) and strips telegram_ids — the admin sees how many users match, never a list of ids. Server re-resolves the audience on Send and refuses count 0. Service picker: search by id/name, exact-id first, top 20.
- **Session gate:** admin_authenticated=true, same as admin-confused-chats.php. English only, compact dense form/table, brand #3275dd. Photo attach (JPG/PNG/GIF/WebP, max 5 MB, px-4650dbff/uploads/) and schedule option kept from existing Broadcasting.
- **SEND STEP — NOT WIRED (assumption, see H1 in the file):** the page does NOT send Telegram itself (no bot token in the admin panel). On Send it records the broadcast in a new table bot_broadcasts (audience_segment, service_id, service_name, message, photo_path, audience_count, delivered, failed, status, send_at, sent_at, created_at) as 'queued'/'scheduled', then calls tb_dispatch_to_existing_sender() — a stub that reports "not wired" until the installer connects it to the existing Broadcasting send mechanism (NOT in this repo — its name/file could not be written here). Until wired, nothing is sent (safe failure mode). The wired sender must fetch the audience via the same endpoint, send, then UPDATE the row (status 'sent', sent_at, delivered, failed). Deleting a sent message stays with the existing sender (needs per-user Telegram message ids). If the live Broadcasting already has a history table, merge instead of running two (H2).

No PHP lint run here (no PHP in the sandbox) — `php -l` must run on the server at install.
- **Still needed:** upload to px-4650dbff/ + nav link + php -l + wire tb_dispatch_to_existing_sender() + verify V1–V8 in the file header (admin folder, login URL, session key, db.php, config_secret.php, back link, shared layout, photo folder) + curl test for the audience count (see file header). - **Built by subagent 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (offline only). Four NEW files in `server/`, no existing file edited, no push/deploy/live install.** Each mirrors `api-user-traits.php` exactly (header contract, CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS, X-Bot-Secret auth, `bot_ok()` envelope, dispatch lines + live curl test in the footer). `php -l` NOT run here (no PHP in the workspace) — must run on the server at install, per file + `php -l api/bot.php` after splicing.
- **Promo codes — `server/api-promo-codes.php` (693 lines).** Tables `bot_promo_codes` (code PK, percent 1–100, active, expires_at, max_uses) + `bot_promo_redemptions` (UNIQUE telegram_id+code, UNIQUE deposit_id). Endpoints: POST `/promo-codes/validate` {telegram_id, code} → {ok, percent, message, already_used} (business failures are HTTP 200 ok:false; already-redeemed is "already used", not an error); POST `/promo-codes/redeem` {telegram_id, code, deposit_id} → {ok, bonus_etb, percent, code, deposit_id, duplicate} — deposit must be completed and the caller's, bonus = amount × percent credited once in a transaction, idempotent by deposit_id; POST `/promo-codes` {code, percent, …} creates a code, gated by X-Bot-Secret for the ADMIN BOT to call after William confirms (same gate as the settings endpoint — investigated in api-admin-settings.php; human-panel session auth is for browser pages, not this). Hook: `prc_maybe_redeem()` runs inside the caller's transaction, called from `bot_dep_status()` by a one-line manual patch (same place as the referral hook) plus a one-line patch to `bot_dep_create()` to carry `promo_code` in `chapa_response` JSON — exact patch lines in the file. Money rule: a code never changes prices, never creates money from nothing — bonus only on a real completed deposit, once per user, once per deposit, Paxyo balance never cash. Assumptions: deposits/auth columns as in api-deposits.php; `bot_dep_status()` is the only bot-API credit path — website deposits never carry a code and get no automatic bonus (manual redeem is the fallback); no max-bonus cap (a 100% code doubles a deposit — William only deliberately). Open: who creates codes and where (admin bot chosen; panel needs server-side call or the SQL INSERT in the footer), list/deactivate UI, max-bonus cap.
- **Rate your order — `server/api-order-ratings.php` (349 lines).** Table `bot_order_ratings` (order_id PK, telegram_id, service_id, rating good|bad). Endpoints: POST `/orders/{id}/rate` {telegram_id, rating} → {ok, order_id, rating} — only COMPLETED orders belonging to the caller (other users' orders get the same 404; ownership is checked before any rating state is revealed), 409 if already rated (server enforces once, bot never asks twice); GET `/ratings/services?service_ids=1,2,3` → {ratings: {id: {good, bad}}} — unknown ids return zero counts, never 500; max 100 ids. The bot-side demotion is a separate build — the server only stores + exposes counts. Dispatch note: the `orders/{id}/rate` pattern must be checked before the generic `orders/{id}` handler. Assumption: only status 'completed' counts as finished.
- **Report a problem — `server/api-support-reports.php` (380 lines).** INVESTIGATED FIRST: no server endpoint/table for support messages exists anywhere in this repo — today the bot routes support intent to the t.me/paxyo contact line and the user messages William directly; the admin's Support Chat section has no local file/table. So this is a NEW table `bot_support_reports` (id, telegram_id, username best-effort from auth, order_id NULL, message max 2000 chars, status open|handled|closed), not a wrapper. Endpoints: POST `/support-reports` {telegram_id, order_id?, message} → {ok, report_id, status:"open", note:"…does not refund your order…"} (order_id nullable; when given it must belong to the caller); GET `/support-reports?status=open` → {reports, count}, newest first, max 50, optional &telegram_id= filter, every row carrying order id, user and message. THIS FLOW NEVER REFUNDS — header + response note. No status-change endpoint yet (SQL in the footer); no admin list page built (GET endpoint only).
- **Refund from the admin bot — `server/api-admin-refund.php` (470 lines).** INVESTIGATED FIRST: @paxadmin_bot is Abel's, different codebase, NOT in this repo — nothing can be added to it from here. The refund action already exists (api-order-rescue.php POST `/orders/{id}/cancel-refund`), so this is a THIN ADMIN-GATED WRAPPER with no refund logic of its own. Endpoint: POST `/admin/orders/{id}/refund` {telegram_id (order owner), admin_telegram_id, confirm_token} → exactly the cancel-refund reply. Gate: X-Bot-Secret + confirm_token = HMAC-SHA256 keyed by BOT_API_SECRET over `"admin-refund:{order_id}:{admin_telegram_id}"` (bound to one order + one admin; only mintable after William's Confirm tap); optional server-side allowlist constant ADMIN_BOT_TELEGRAM_IDS. One-tap-from-alert NEEDS ABEL (alerts go to @paxadmin_bot) or a decision to route alerts to the new admin bot — until then William types the command. Install api-order-rescue.php FIRST (wrapper 503s without it). Assumption: `bot_ok()` returns rather than exits (evidence in-repo; if it exits, the refund is still correct and only the audit row stays 'requested' — reconcile via bot_order_rescues).

## 15 Nov 08:xx EAT — Ideas batch, feature 5: "You usually order this on Fridays" (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **What it does.** The bot notices a weekly ordering pattern — the SAME service + quantity ordered on the SAME EAT weekday at least twice (from the server GET orders list, via the existing `PaxyoClient.recent_orders`, the same call `handle_usual` uses) — and when the user messages on that weekday again, offers it once: "You usually order 1000 TikTok views on Fridays — want me to place it?" At most one offer per pattern per week (key = telegram_id + service_id + quantity + weekday + ISO week, in memory). Accepting ("Yes, place it" button, or typing yes) pre-fills the draft with the pattern's service + quantity and asks for the link; the last matching order's link is offered as a "Same link as last time" button only — never reused silently. The normal Confirm card still gates the order; nothing auto-places. Declining (button or "no" / "not today") ends it quietly with no nagging and no re-offer that week.
- **Where the check runs and why.** In `handle_order_text`, right after intent extraction, gated to light intents only (`greeting`, `off_topic`, `unclear`) — NOT the 60-second scheduler: no wake-up spam, no message to a user who is not chatting, one orders-list call per light message. Order messages never reach the gate (they are `order`/`usual`/`batch`/…), so an order in progress is never hijacked. The offer never fires while a draft or any Confirm card is open, never when the same service + quantity was already ordered this ISO week, and never while an offer is already open. An offer ignored last week is dropped as stale so it cannot block next week's offer.
- **What changed — bot.py.** Templates `friday_offer`, `friday_yes_button`, `friday_no_button`, `friday_link_ask`, `friday_link_ask_same`, `friday_same_link_button`, `friday_declined`, `friday_gone` (en + first-pass am, unreviewed; buttons stay English like Confirm cards). `_WEEKDAY_NAMES` (en/am, Monday 0 … Sunday 6) next to `EAT_TZ`. State `FRIDAY_OFFERED` (set of weekly keys), `FRIDAY_PENDING` (open offer per user, incl. `offered_week`), `FRIDAY_LINK` (last link, set only on accept; popped by `clear_order_state`). Pure helpers `_parse_order_created_at` (ISO-with-Z or MySQL DATETIME, as UTC; unparseable → None) and `detect_weekly_patterns` (groups by service + quantity + EAT weekday, ≥2; newest-first, carrying the newest order's name/link). `maybe_friday_pattern_offer` (the check above), `friday_offer_keyboard`, `_friday_accept_text` (validates via `resolve_named_service`, pre-fills PARTIAL, pins the service one-shot via `USUAL_SERVICE` like the usual fallback, prices from the catalogue), `_friday_accept_reply`, `is_friday_decline`. Hooks: the offer gate in `handle_order_text` after the order-override; the "yes" branch last in the bare-acceptance block (a "yes" to a real Confirm card/plan/batch/receipt always wins); the typed-decline check before the free-chat branch ("no thanks" would otherwise be swallowed as small talk); `on_callback` branches `friday` (accept), `fridayno` (decline), `fridaylink` (same link → `complete_order` with the named service id; the one-shot pin is dropped first so it can never hijack a later order).
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** New `TestFridayPatterns` (19 tests): detection (two same-weekday orders → pattern with the newest link; one order / quantity differs / weekday differs → no pattern; 22:30 UTC counts as Saturday in EAT; dateless orders ignored), offer (fires on a greeting on the pattern weekday with both buttons; once per week; not on a different weekday; not when already ordered this week; not with a draft or a Confirm card open; fires again next week; Amharic offer), accept (typed yes pre-fills service + quantity, asks for the link with the catalogue price, places nothing, shows no card yet; link then shows the Confirm card; button + same-link tap shows the Confirm card with the old link), decline (button and typed "no thanks" both end quietly, no re-offer). **No existing test was changed.**
- **BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (1071/1071 tests: 1052 baseline + 19 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed.**
- **Caveats:** in-memory only — a restart may re-offer (same caveat as the delivery watch); the offer fires on light messages only (greeting/off-topic/unclear), so a user who only ever sends order messages on that weekday never sees it; Amharic templates are first-pass, unreviewed.
- **Open questions:** (a) should the offer also fire from the scheduler (e.g. a Friday-morning proactive message) later, or stay message-triggered only? Builder recommends staying message-triggered — scheduler offers would message users who aren't chatting. (b) `recent_orders` returns max 20 orders — a user whose two same-weekday orders are older than their last 20 orders won't get the offer; a wider history needs a server change.

## 15 Nov 08:xx EAT — Admin bot, batch 2: refund from the admin bot + promo-code creation (built, verified offline, NOT pushed/deployed/installed)
- **What it does (William only, English only).** (1) REFUND — "refund order #1841" (also "refund 1841" / "refund order 1841" / "refund #1841") → the bot looks the order up (GET /orders/{id}), shows id / service / quantity / total / status / owner telegram id, William taps Confirm, and only then it mints the HMAC confirm token and calls POST /admin/orders/{id}/refund. The full total goes back to the user's Paxyo balance (never cash), once, never twice, never for a completed order. (2) PROMO CODES — "create promo code PAXYO20 20%" (also "new promo code WELCOME10 10 percent") → preview of the code + percent (+ expiry / max uses when given, e.g. "expires 2026-12-31", "max 100 uses"), Confirm, then POST /promo-codes.
- **What changed — admin_bot.py.** New client methods: `get_order(order_id)` (GET /orders/{id}, 404 → None — the admin bot had no single-order read; the stats/broadcast paths could not supply the owner's telegram id), `admin_refund_order(order_id, telegram_id, admin_telegram_id, confirm_token)` (POST /admin/orders/{id}/refund, errors raise for the caller to map), `create_promo_code(code, percent, expires_at?, max_uses?)` (POST /promo-codes, 404 → None). Pure helpers: `mint_admin_refund_token` (HMAC-SHA256 keyed by BOT_API_SECRET over exactly "admin-refund:{order_id}:{admin_telegram_id}", minted only after Confirm, never logged); `parse_refund_command`; `parse_promo_create_command` (code uppercased; expiry sent as "YYYY-MM-DD 23:59:59"); `validate_promo_create` (code letters+digits 4–20 chars, percent 1–100, expiry a real future date, max uses ≥ 1 — refused plainly before any Confirm); `_order_record` / `_order_owner` / `_order_total` / `_order_service_label` (records read tolerantly: flat or nested under "order", owner from telegram_id/user_id/tg_id); `_refund_error_text` / `_promo_error_text` (plain server messages per server/api-admin-refund.php). `_execute_pending` gained "refund" and "promo" branches (pending cleared before executing, so a double Confirm runs once). `on_text` parses refund/promo deterministically after stats/broadcast/ticker and before the AI — broadcasts and tickers are parsed first, so a broadcast message or ticker text mentioning "refund order #N" or "create promo code" is read as the broadcast/ticker it is. The AI is never called for either action (test-proven). Safety line updated: the docstring and CAPABILITIES no longer say the bot "never touches refunds" — they say refunds go through the Confirm-gated admin-refund endpoint, once, never twice, never for completed orders, only to Paxyo balance, never cash; /start and the fallback reply list both new commands.
- **Exact user-visible behaviour.** Refund preview: "Refund this order? Order: #1841 / Service: TikTok Video Views ×1000 / Total: 10.00 ETB / Status: pending / User: 779060335 / The full total goes back to the user's Paxyo balance (not cash). Tap Confirm to refund it, or Cancel." Completed order: "Order #1841 is already completed and cannot be refunded." (no Confirm offered). Success: "✅ Refunded 10.00 ETB to user 779060335 for order #1841 (Paxyo balance, not cash). Provider: canceled." Duplicate: "Already refunded — 10.00 ETB for order #1841. No second refund was made." 409 already-completed: "Order #1841 is already completed and cannot be refunded." 409/502 provider-refused: "No refund was made — {error}." verbatim. POST 404 with body "Order not found.": "Order not found." POST 404 with any other body: "The refund endpoint is not connected yet — nothing was refunded." 403: "Confirm token is not valid." Lookup 404: "The order lookup is not connected yet — I couldn't look up order #1841." Promo preview: "Create this promo code? Code: PAXYO20 / Bonus: 20% on a deposit / Expires: 2026-12-31 / Max uses: 100 / Tap Confirm to create it, or Cancel." Success: "✅ Promo code PAXYO20 created — 20% bonus on a deposit." (+ expiry/max lines when set). 409: the server's duplicate message shown plainly ("A promo code with that code already exists."). 404: "The promo-code endpoint is not connected yet — nothing was created."
- **What changed — test_admin_bot_offline.py.** New `TestRefundParsing` (4), `TestRefundFlow` (19), `TestPromoParsing` (5), `TestPromoValidation` (8), `TestPromoFlow` (15), `TestSafetyLine` (3) — 54 new tests. `FakeAdminPaxyo` gained seeded orders, `get_order` / `admin_refund_order` / `create_promo_code` mirrors (HTTP errors crafted as `httpx.HTTPStatusError` with a JSON error body, mirroring test_bot_offline.py; `create_promo_code` mirrors the real client: 404 → None). Token test computes the expected HMAC independently from the test secret and asserts 64 hex chars + binding to one order + one admin. **No existing test was updated** — no existing test asserted the old "never touches refunds" line (verified by grep; it appeared only in admin_bot.py); the new line is pinned by the 3 new `TestSafetyLine` tests. One real bug caught by the tests: the percent regex's trailing `\b` never matched after "%" (non-word char + space has no word boundary) — fixed by dropping it.
- **BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (125/125 admin tests: 71 baseline + 54 new; test_bot_offline.py 1108/1108 OK on the final run — see the count caveat below). NOT pushed, NOT deployed, NOT installed.**
- **Count caveat:** test_bot_offline.py read 1071 at this build's baseline, then 1071 with 7 failures on one mid-task run, then 1108/1108 OK. This build never touched bot.py or test_bot_offline.py (that suite has no reference to admin_bot); bot.py and test_bot_offline.py were modified at 05:54 UTC by the sibling coordinator build running concurrently, and the 7-failure run coincided with its mid-edit state.
- **Still needed:** BotFather token (→ Render ADMIN_BOT_TOKEN); server installs (splice server/api-admin-refund.php AFTER server/api-order-rescue.php — the wrapper 503s without it — and server/api-promo-codes.php + its two manual hook patches; `php -l`; footer curl tests, small test order/deposit only, under the 5 ETB cap, with William's approval); Render service for admin_bot.py. Until the endpoints are live, the bot answers "not connected yet" and refunds/creates nothing.
- **Open questions / caveats:** (a) live GET /orders/{id} is user-scoped (`?telegram_id=` per bot.py) and the admin bot has no owner id before the read — `get_order` calls it bare per the refund spec; whether the live handler answers that is unverified, and an admin read path may be needed server-side. (b) POST 404 is ambiguous (endpoint-not-installed vs order-not-found) — the bot distinguishes by body text ("Order not found." vs anything else); confirm the live bot.php 404 fallback's error string at install time. (c) Preview 404 is ambiguous the same way — a mistyped order id reads as "not connected yet". (d) The server's 409 for a completed order says "cannot be cancelled." while the spec's user line says "cannot be refunded." — the bot maps on "already completed" to the spec wording.
- **First-use password gate (15 Nov 11:59 EAT, built, verified offline, NOT pushed/deployed).** William asked for a password as well as the allowlist, because a Telegram bot cannot be made private/hidden. New env var `ADMIN_BOT_PASSWORD` — no default, no hardcoded fallback; unset/empty = gate off and behaviour is exactly the allowlist-only behaviour. When set, an allowlisted user must enter the password once per process run before ANY command, question, broadcast or setting change is answered (commands get one short prompt line; a text message is treated as a password attempt — correct unlocks, wrong gets a short refusal, 5 wrong in an hour locks that user out for an hour, in-memory). The correct password unlocks that user id for the rest of the process run; a redeploy/restart asks again. Non-allowlisted users get the existing refusal and never reach the prompt. The password is never logged, echoed or stored anywhere except the env var; comparison is constant-time (`hmac.compare_digest`). Confirm steps unchanged. New `TestPasswordGate` (13 tests); admin suite now 170/170. Still needed: William chooses the password himself (via secure form) and it goes into Render as `ADMIN_BOT_PASSWORD` when the admin bot is deployed. DEPLOY FIX 15 Nov 12:17 EAT: the first Render deploy of atlas-desk-bot crashed (Python 3.14: `RuntimeError: There is no current event loop in thread 'MainThread'` at `run_polling()`); fixed with `_ensure_event_loop()` in `main()` + a regression test that reproduces the crash in a fresh thread; admin suite 171/171.

## 15 Nov 08:xx EAT — Ideas batch 2, feature 1: promo codes in chat (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **Built by subagent 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (offline only). `bot.py` + `test_bot_offline.py` only; `HANDOVER.md`, `admin_bot.py`, `server/` untouched. BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (1108/1108 tests: 1071 baseline + 37 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed, NOT installed.** Server side (`server/api-promo-codes.php` + hook patches) still to install — until then the bot answers "promo codes aren't switched on yet" and no deposit carries a code.
- **What changed — bot.py.** `PaxyoClient.validate_promo` (POST /promo-codes/validate) + `redeem_promo` (POST /promo-codes/redeem), both 404-tolerant like save_session (endpoint not live yet → None + warning, never raise into a chat); business failures (HTTP 200 ok:false, incl. already_used) pass through, never raise. `create_deposit(..., promo_code=None)` — the code rides on the body only when given. State: `VALIDATED_PROMO` (validated code, one deposit only), `PROMO_DEPOSIT` (code attached to a created deposit), `PROMO_OFFER` (one-shot "Is this a promo code?" question). Helpers: `extract_promo_code` (bare token or "my code is X", letters+digits, 4–20 chars; bare numbers/words are not codes), `extract_promo_offer` (code-shaped token in a short otherwise-unmatched message), `_promo_busy` (any draft open or Confirm card pending blocks detection), `handle_promo_code` (validate + answer + remember), `handle_promo_text` (invite-code tokens get the invite-link reply, never a promo "does not exist"), `_promo_bonus_line` (redeem safety net on completion). Detection runs before the classifier, after the paused-services guard; the offer's yes/no/stale handling runs just before the acceptance block. Templates (en + first-pass am): `promo_accepted`, `promo_already_used`, `promo_invalid`, `promo_inactive`, `promo_expired`, `promo_used_up`, `promo_not_live`, `promo_ask`, `promo_no`, `promo_bonus`, `promo_bonus_applied`, `promo_bonus_failed`, `deposit_confirm_promo`.
- **Flow.** User types a code → validate first; success confirms the bonus ("you'll get 20% extra on your next deposit") and remembers it for ONE deposit; already-used / invalid / expired get the server's own reason in the user's language, never an invented one; 404 gets the honest not-switched-on line. The deposit Confirm card shows the promo line when the code was validated before the card was built. `start_deposit` threads the code into the create call (server hook credits the bonus on completion); on success the code moves to PROMO_DEPOSIT — a failed create keeps it, so "Try again" still carries it; a second deposit carries nothing and needs the code typed again. On completion, `deposit_status_text` ("I paid", "I paid — check it", receipt recheck) appends the bonus line: the redeem reply's exact figure when available (redeem returns duplicate:true with the recorded bonus when the hook already paid — never a second credit), the plain "applied with this deposit" line when redeem 404s, an honest "couldn't be added" line when redeem clearly fails; the code is cleared either way. Website deposits work too: a validated code + a completed website deposit → "I paid" redeems it against that deposit (redeem 404 there keeps the code for the next deposit instead of dropping it). A code never discounts an order price — bonus on a real completed deposit only, once per user, once per deposit.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** New `TestPromoCodesApiClient` (9 tests: validate/redeem 404 → None, request bodies, ok:false + already_used + duplicate passthrough, non-404 raises, create_deposit with/without promo_code) and `TestPromoCodes` (28 tests: bare/lowercase/"my code is" validation + remembering, invalid/expired/already-used/not-live/error answers, bare number/word not a code, code-shaped token inside an order + code while an order/deposit draft is open do not hijack, offer ask + yes validates + no declines + stale message drops it + "deposit PAXYO20" never offers, invite code gets the invite reply, Confirm card shows the code, create carries the code, second deposit has none, completion clears the code and mentions the exact bonus, website-deposit redeem, redeem-not-live applied line, Amharic ask + accept, detection/failure-text unit cases). `FakePaxyo` gained `validate_promo`/`redeem_promo` mirrors (unmapped codes get the "does not exist" record; valid ones must be seeded) + `promo_codes`/`promo_none`/`promo_redeem_result`/`promo_redeem_none`/`promo_error` params, and `create_deposit` records `promo_code` as sent. **No existing test was updated.**
- **One real bug caught by the new tests (fixed in this build):** typing `PAXYO20` while a deposit draft was open didn't validate the code (correct) but `parse_deposit_amount` read the "20" inside the token and silently rewrote the draft's amount (100 → 20). The mid-deposit follow-up now skips promo-code-shaped messages, leaving the draft exactly as it was.
- **Flake (not a bug in this build):** the first full run after the edits failed 1/1108 — `test_history_persists_to_file` (`assertTrue(os.path.exists(...))`). It passes in isolation, passes with the promo classes, and the next two full runs were 1108/1108 clean with identical code; no history code was touched. Looks like a pre-existing order/timing flake in that test (shared temp filename across classes + the 5s save throttle) — watch it, don't chase it.
- **Open questions:** (a) a code typed while a deposit is already being built is ignored — should validating mid-deposit be allowed (remember it without touching the draft, so it attaches to the in-progress deposit)? (b) a validated code is forgotten on restart (in-memory only) — persist it in the session store, or leave it? (c) a code validated after the Confirm card was built still attaches at creation but the card won't show the promo line — rebuild the card or leave it? (d) Amharic promo templates are first-pass, unreviewed by a native speaker.
- **Still to verify live (after the server install + deploy):** "PAXYO20" → accepted + remembered; an invalid code → honest invalid line; a deposit with the code → bonus lands on completion and the "I paid" reply names the exact bonus figure; a second deposit without retyping the code → no bonus; a website deposit after a validated code → bonus via the redeem path.

## 15 Nov 08:xx EAT — Ideas batch 2, feature 2: report a problem in chat (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **Built by subagent 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (offline only). `bot.py` + `test_bot_offline.py` only; `HANDOVER.md`, `admin_bot.py`, `server/` untouched. BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (1141/1141 tests: 1108 baseline + 33 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed, NOT installed.** Server side (`server/api-support-reports.php`) still to install — until then the bot answers "I couldn't file that right now — please message our support at t.me/paxyo with order #N and what happened" and files nothing.
- **What changed — bot.py.** `PaxyoClient.report_problem(telegram_id, message, order_id=None)` (POST /support-reports, order_id nullable; 404 → None + warning, other errors raise). State `REPORT_PENDING` (telegram_id -> {"order_id", "step": "pick"|"describe"}). Helpers: `is_report_problem_request`, `is_report_complaint`, `extract_report_order_id` ("order 1841", "order #1841", "#1841", bare digits; a digit run followed by a metric word is a quantity, not an id — "order 10k views" asks which order instead of filing against #10000), `extract_quoted_order_id` ("order #1839" in the delivery text, "Order ID: 1839" in the summary; a Confirm card names no id), `report_pick_keyboard`, `_report_ask_which`, `_report_ask_description`, `_report_file` (pops the flow before filing, trims to the server's 2000-char cap), `handle_report_text`. `reorder_keyboard` adds one "⚠️ Report a problem — #<id>" row per order after the reorder rows (callback `report:<order_id>`), so the button shows under My orders (/orders + 📦 quick action) and under a placed-order summary. `on_callback` "report" branch: tap starts the flow asking what's wrong (order id when tapped, none for "report:none"); the id is NOT validated against recent orders — the server checks ownership at filing. Templates (en + first-pass am): `report_ask_order`, `report_ask_no_order`, `report_ask_which`, `report_ask_which_no_orders`, `report_filed`, `report_filed_no_order`, `report_not_live`, `report_not_live_no_order`, `report_cancelled`, `report_busy`, `report_no_order_button` (en-only).
- **Flow.** (a) Button tap → "What's wrong with order #N?" (b) Typed "report a problem with order 1841" / "my order 1841 didn't arrive" / "there's a problem with my order" — deterministic, before the classifier; with an id it asks for the description, without one it asks which order (recent orders as buttons, typed id, or "no order"). (c) A complaint replied to a delivery/order message ("it never arrived", "wrong", "stuck", "missing") starts the flow for the quoted order; "thanks!" and "where is it" do not. One description message files the report; the confirmation names the order and says it does NOT refund ("if you need a refund, talk to support at t.me/paxyo"). Endpoint not live (404) or any filing error → the honest t.me/paxyo fallback, never a pretence it was filed. "cancel" drops the flow with nothing filed; a bare "yes" as a description re-asks; a quick-reply tap mid-flow drops the flow and runs normally.
- **Hijack rule (safer option chosen): the report flow NEVER starts while an order, deposit, plan or Confirm card is open** (reuses `_promo_busy`) — the user finishes or cancels that first ("You have an order in progress — finish it or tap Cancel first, then tell me what's wrong."). While the flow is open every message is read as its answer, so no draft can start underneath it.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** New `TestReportProblemApiClient` (4 tests: 404 → None, request bodies with/without order id, 500 raises) and `TestReportProblem` (29 tests: detection/extraction/complaint unit cases, Amharic templates + bridged Amharic request, button on a My-orders row, typed requests, description filed with the right order id + user + message, exact confirmation text, ask-which-order incl. no-recent-orders text, typed id in pick step, pick-step button tap, no-order typed and via `report:none`, not-live/error honesty with and without an order, cancel in both steps, bare-"yes" re-ask, quick-reply tap mid-flow, draft and Confirm card not hijacked, reply-to-delivery complaint and thanks, plain "my orders" is not a report, "order 10k views" is not an order id). `FakePaxyo` gained a `report_problem` mirror (`reports_filed`, `report_none`, `report_error`). **One existing test updated deliberately:** `test_reorder_keyboard_shape` — row count 2 → 4, with assertions pinning the new report rows; the reorder rows themselves are unchanged.
- **One real bug caught by the new tests (fixed in this build):** `extract_report_order_id` first returned 10000 for "my order 10k views didn't arrive" — `normalize_text` expands "10k" to "10000" before the extractor runs, defeating the "k"-suffix guard; the extractor now also rejects a digit run followed by a metric word (views/followers/likes/...).
- **Caveats:** the filed message is the normalized (lowercased) text, like the confusion log — original capitalisation is lost. The 404 is ambiguous by design (endpoint-not-live vs. order-not-yours); both get the same honest fallback. A typed order id is not pre-validated against history — the server is the authority. Photo descriptions are not accepted (text only). A stale Confirm-card tap while a report flow is open could still place that order via callback.
- **Open questions:** (a) queue a report request for after a draft finishes, instead of refusing while one is open? (b) pre-validate a typed order id against the user's history before asking for the description? (c) accept a photo (e.g. failure screenshot) as the description? (d) Amharic templates are first-pass, unreviewed.
- **Still to verify live (after the server install + deploy):** tap "⚠️ Report a problem" on a My-orders row → ask → describe → filed + confirmation naming the order and the no-refund line; typed "my order 1841 didn't arrive" → same; "there's a problem with my order" → pick list → tap → file; report without an order → filed with order_id null; before the install, the same turns answer the honest t.me/paxyo fallback; "cancel" drops the flow; a report typed mid-order gets the busy line and the draft survives.

## 15 Nov 08:xx EAT — Ideas batch 2, feature 3: rate your order in chat (built, verified, NOT pushed/deployed yet)
- **Built by subagent 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (offline only). `bot.py` + `test_bot_offline.py` only; `HANDOVER.md`, `admin_bot.py`, `server/` untouched. BUILT + VERIFIED offline 15 Nov 08:xx EAT (1173/1173 tests: 1141 baseline + 32 new). NOT pushed, NOT deployed, NOT installed.** Server side (`server/api-order-ratings.php`) still to install — until then taps answer "Ratings aren't switched on yet" and nothing is filed.
- **What changed — bot.py.** `PaxyoClient.rate_order(order_id, telegram_id, rating)` (POST `/orders/{id}/rate`, 404 → None + warning, 409 passed through as `{"ok": false, "already_rated": bool, "error": msg}` — True for already-rated, False for not-completed — other errors raise) and `service_ratings(service_ids)` (GET `/ratings/services?service_ids=…` → `{id: {"good", "bad"}}`, 404 → None, empty list → `{}` without a call, trimmed to 100 ids). State `RATING_ASKED` (ask attached) + `RATED_ORDERS` (rated this process). `rating_keyboard(order_id)` (👍/👎, `rate:<id>:good|bad`). `apply_rating_demotions(candidates, ratings)` (pure), `pick_services(..., ratings=None)`, `pick_services_rated(...)` (one cached ratings call per pick at most, 5-min cache, any failure ranks as today), `_cached_service_ratings`. Templates (en + first-pass am): `rate_ask`, `rate_thanks_good`, `rate_thanks_bad`, `rate_already`, `rate_not_live`, `rate_not_completed`, `rate_failed`.
- **Flow.** A completed order's delivery message carries the ask once (line + 👍/👎 buttons); never asked twice, never nagged — an ignored ask never re-appears on later messages. One tap files the rating and appends one short line ("Thanks — noted. 👍" / "Sorry it wasn't great — noted, we'll use better services next time."); a second tap answers "Already noted — thanks." and files nothing; the server 409 is the backstop for a rating filed from another device. Endpoint not live → honest "Ratings aren't switched on yet."; a failed filing leaves the order unmarked so a retry tap can file. A 👎 is feedback only — it never refunds (a refund needs a report or support). Reply language = the user's language (`LAST_LANG`); the delivery message itself stays English, like all scheduler notices.
- **Demotion rule (bot side; the server only stores + exposes counts).** bad ≥ 10 → skipped for this pick, unless it is the only candidate left (never no service; if all are 10+ bad, all are kept fastest-first). bad ≥ 3 AND bad > good → demoted below every non-demoted service (same speed or slower; demoted services keep fastest-first order among themselves). 1–2 bad, or more good than bad → untouched. Never changes a price, never re-enables a blacklisted service (5379/5834 excluded before ranking), never applies to cheapest-first or reaction-emoji picks. Wired into `complete_order`'s fastest pick only — the single funnel for normal/order-now/reorder/Friday/nudge orders; named, pinned and last-order services are never demoted. The Confirm card still shows the picked service + price, unchanged.
- **What changed — test_bot_offline.py.** New `TestRateOrderApiClient` (9 tests) and `TestRateOrder` (23 tests): client shape/404/409 passthrough (both 409 kinds)/raises; demotion unit cases (3+ bad below same-speed clean, 2 bad untouched, more-good untouched, 10+ bad skipped/lone-kept/all-kept, blacklist excluded, prices unchanged, cheapest untouched); rated pick fetches once + cache reuse, ratings failure changes nothing, client without the method; delivery message carries the buttons + asks once; tap good/bad, tap twice files once, not-live honesty, already-rated 409, error + retry; ignored ask never re-appears; `complete_order` demotes the bad-rated fastest pick; Amharic templates. `FakePaxyo` gained `rate_order`/`service_ratings` mirrors (`ratings_filed`, `ratings_calls`, `ratings`/`ratings_none`/`rate_none`/`rate_error`/`rate_already` seeds). **No existing test was updated.**
- **Caveats:** in-memory only — a restart loses RATING_ASKED/RATED_ORDERS and the ratings cache, but the watch is in-memory too, so no ask is ever sent twice for the same order. Demotion is only in `complete_order`'s pick — batch orders, the stall-rescue re-pick, cross-sell and price/speed answers still use the undemoted pick. A bad-rated service drops below ALL clean services, not only same-speed ones. Amharic templates are first-pass, unreviewed.
- **Open questions:** (a) should demotion be same-speed-only instead of below-all-clean? (b) should batch orders / rescue re-pick / cross-sell also demote? (c) should a 👎 ever offer "report a problem" as a follow-up, or stay feedback-only?
- **Still to verify live (after the server install + deploy):** complete an order → delivery message carries 👍/👎; tap 👍 → "Thanks — noted." and the count shows good:1; tap 👎 on another order → sorry line and bad:1; tap twice → "Already noted", one filing; a 3+ bad service ranks below a same-speed clean one on the next Confirm card and a 10+ bad one is skipped when a clean alternative exists; before the install, taps answer "Ratings aren't switched on yet."
